agniśca jātavedaśca | sahojā ajirāprabhuḥ | vaiśvānaro naryāpāśca | paṅktirādhāśca saptamaḥ | visarpevā'ṣṭamo'gnīnām | ete'ṣṭau vasavaḥ kṣitā iti, iti | yathartvevāgnerarcirvarṇaviśeṣāḥ | nīlārciśca pītakārciśceti, iti | atha vāyorekādaśapuruṣasyaikādaśastrīkasya, iti | prabhrājamānā vyavadātāḥ  | yāśca vāsukivaidyutāḥ | rajatāḥ paruṣāḥ śyāmāḥ | kapilā atilohitāḥ | ūrdhvā avapatantāśca | vaidyuta ityekādaśa, iti | nainaṃ vaidyuto hinasti | ya evaṃ veda, iti | sa hovāca vyāsaḥ pārāśaryaḥ | vidyudvadhamevāhaṃ mṛtyumaicchamiti, iti | na tvakāmaṃ hanti  | ya evaṃ veda, iti | atha gandharvagaṇāḥ, iti | svānabhrāṭ | aṅghārirbambhāriḥ | hastaḥ suhastaḥ | kṛśānurviśvāvasuḥ | mūrdhanvānasūryavarcāḥ | kṛtirityekādaśa gandharvagaṇāḥ, iti | devāśca mahādevāḥ | raśmayaśca devā garagiraḥ , iti | nainaṃ garo hinasti | ya evaṃ veda, iti | gaurī mimāya salilāni takṣatī | ekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī | aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūbuṣī | sahasrākṣarā parame vyomanniti, iti | vāco viśeṣaṇam, iti | atha nigadavyākhyātāḥ | tānanukramiṣyāmaḥ, iti | varāhavaḥ svatapasaḥ  | vidyunmahaso dhūpayaḥ | śvāpayo gṛhamedhāścetyete | ye ceme'śimividviṣaḥ, iti | parjanyāḥ sapta pṛthivīmabhivarṣanti | vṛṣṭibhiriti | etayaiva vibhaktiviparītāḥ | saptabhirvātairudīritāḥ | amūm̐llokānabhivarṣanti | teṣāmeṣā bhavati, iti | samānametadudakam  | uccaityava cāhabhiḥ | bhūmiṃ parjanyā jinvanti | divaṃ jinvantyagnaya iti, iti | yadakṣaraṃ bhūtakṛtam | viśve devā upāsate | maharṣimasya goptāram | jamadagnimakurvata, iti | jamadagnirāpyāyate | chandobhiścaturuttaraiḥ | rājñaḥ somasya tṛptāsaḥ  | brahmaṇā vīryāvatā | śivā naḥ pradiśo diśaḥ, iti | tacchaṃ yorāvṛṇīmahe | gātuṃ yajñāya | gātuṃ yajñapataye | daivī svastirastu naḥ | svastirmānuṣebhyaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ jigātu bheṣajam | śaṃ no astu dvipade| śaṃ catuṣpade, iti | somapā3 asomapā3 iti nigadavyākhyātāḥ , iti | vyavadātā hanti garagirastatapasa udakaṃ tṛptāsaścatuṣpada ekaṃ ca ||

uṣmā ca nīhāraśca | vṛtrasyoṣmā | vaidyutasya nīhāraḥ | tau tāveva pratipadyete, iti | seyaṃ rātrī garbhiṇī putreṇa saṃvasati | tasyā vā etadulbaṇam | yadrātrau raśmayaḥ | yathā gorgarbhiṇyā ulbaṇam | evametasyāṃ ulbaṇam, iti | prajayiṣṇuḥ prajayā ca paśubhiśca bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | etamudyantamapiyantaṃ ceti | ādityaḥ puṇyasya vatsaḥ, iti | atha pavitrāṅgirasaḥ , iti || stho'podakābhirvaśī dadhāthe agnistayoretau vatsau bhavati catvāri ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake daśamo'nuvākaḥ || 10 ||
pavitravantaḥ pari vājamāsate | pitaiṣāṃ pratno abhirakṣati vratam | mahaḥ samudraṃ varuṇastirodadhe | dhīrā icchekurdharuṇeṣvārabham, iti | pavitraṃ te vitataṃ brahmaṇaspate | prabhurgātrāṇi paryeṣi viśvataḥ | ataptatanūrna tadāmo aśnute | śṛtāsa idvahantastatsamāśata, iti | brahmā devānām, iti | asataḥ sadye tatakṣuḥ  | ṛṣayaḥ saptātriśca yat | sarve'trayo agastyaśca | nakṣatraiḥ śaṃkṛto'vasan, iti | atha savituḥ śyāvāśvasyāvartikāmasya, iti | amī ya ṛkṣā nihitāsa uccā | naktaṃ dadṛśre kuhaciddiveyuḥ | adabdhāni varuṇasya vratāni | vicākaśaccandramā nakṣatrameti, iti | tatsaviturvareṇyam | bhargo devasya dhīmahi  | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt, iti | tatsaviturvṛṇīmahe | vayaṃ devasya bhojanam | śreṣṭhaṃ sarvadhātamam | turaṃ bhagasya dhīmahi, iti | apāgūhata savitā tṛbhīn | sarvāndivo andhasaḥ | naktaṃ tānyabhavandṛśe | asthyasthnā saṃbhaviṣyāmaḥ, iti | nāma nāmaiva nāma me  | napuṃsakaṃ pumāṃstryasmi | sthāvaro'smyatha jaṅgamaḥ | yaje'yakṣi yaṣṭāhe ca, iti | mayā bhūtānyayakṣata | paśavo mama bhūtāni | anūbandhyo'smyahaṃ vibhuḥ, iti | striyaḥ satīḥ | tā u me puṃsa āhuḥ | paśyadakṣaṇvānna vicetadandhaḥ | kaviryaḥ putraḥ sa imā ciketa  | yastā vi'jānātsavituḥ pitā sat, iti | andho maṇimavindat | tamanaṅgulirāvayat | agrīvaḥ pratyamuñcat | tamajihvā asaścata, iti | ūrdhvamūlamavākchākham | vṛkṣaṃ yo veda saṃprati | na sa jātu janaḥ śraddadhyāt | matyurmā mārayāditiḥ, iti | hasitaṃ ruditaṃ gītam  | vīṇā paṇavalāsitam | mṛtaṃ jīvaṃ ca yatkiṃcit | aṅgāni sneva viddhi tat, iti | atṛṣyaṃstṛṣya dhyāyat | asmājjātā me mithū caran | putro nirṛtyā vaidehaḥ | acetā yaśca cetanaḥ, iti | sa taṃ maṇimavindat | so'naṅgulirāvayat | so'grīvaḥ pratyamuñcat  | so'jihvo asaścata, iti | naitamṛṣiṃ viditvā nagaraṃ praviśet | yadi praviśet | mithau caritvā praviśet | tatsaṃbhavasya vratam, iti | ā tamagne rathaṃ tiṣṭha | ekāśvamekayojanam | ekacakramekadhuram | vātadhrājigatiṃ vibho, iti | na riṣyati na vyathate  | nāsyākṣo yātu sajjati | yacchvetānrohitāṃścāgneḥ | rathe yuktvā'dhitiṣṭhati, iti | ekayā ca daśabhiśca svabhūte | dvābhyāmiṣṭaye viṃśatyā ca | tisṛbhiśca vahase triṃśatā ca | niyudbhirvāyaviha tā vimuñca iti || tatakṣurdhīmahi nāma me ciketa gītaṃ pratyamuñcadvyathate sapta ca ||
iti kṛpṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhaka ekādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 11 ||
ātanuṣva pratanuṣva | uddhamā''dhama saṃdhama | āditye candravarṇānām | garbhamādhehi yaḥ pumān, iti | itaḥ siktaṃ sūryagatam | candramase rasaṃ kṛdhi | vārādaṃ janayāgre'gnim | ya eko rudra ucyate, iti | asaṃkhyātāḥ sahasrāṇi | smaryate na ca dṛśyaṃte  | evametaṃ nibodhata, iti | ā mandrairindra haribhiḥ | yāhi mayūraromabhiḥ | mā tvā kecinnyemurinna pāśinaḥ | dadhanveva tā ihi, iti | mā mandrairindra haribhiḥ | yāmi mayūraromabhiḥ | mā mā kecinnyemurinna pāśinaḥ | nidhanveva tām̐3 imi, iti | aṇubhiśca mahadbhiśca  | nighṛṣvairasamāyutaiḥ | kālairharitvamāpannaiḥ | indrā''yāhi sahasrayuk, iti | agnirvibhrāṣṭivasanaḥ | vāyuḥ śvetasikadrukaḥ | saṃvatsaro viṣūvarṇaiḥ | nityāste'nucarāstava, iti | subrahmaṇyoṃ subrahmaṇyoṃ | indrā''gaccha hariva āgaccha medhātitheḥ | meṣa vṛṣaṇaśvasya mene  | gaurāvaskandinnahalyāyai jāra | kauśikabrāhmaṇa gautamabruvāṇa, iti | aruṇāśvā ihā''gatāḥ | vasavaḥ pṛthivikṣitaḥ | aṣṭau digvāsaso'gnayaḥ | agniśca jātavedāścetyete, iti | tāmrāśvāstāmrarathāḥ | tāmravarṇāstathā'sitāḥ | daṇḍahastāḥ khādagdataḥ | ito rudrāḥ parāṃ gatāḥ |  uktaṃ sthānaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca pura ita, iti | bṛhaspatiśca savitā ca | viśvarūpairihā''gatām | rathenodakavartmanā | apsuṣā iti taddvayoḥ, iti | ukto veṣo vāsāṃsi ca | kālāvayavānāmitaḥ pratījyā | vāsātyā ityaśvinoḥ | ko'ntarikṣe śabdaṃ karotīti | vāsiṣṭho rauhiṇo mīmāṃsāṃ cakre | tasyaiṣā bhavati, iti | vāśreva vidyuditi, iti | brahmaṇa udaraṇamasi | brahmaṇa udīraṇamasi | brahmaṇa āstaraṇamasi | brahmaṇa upastaraṇamasi, iti || dṛśyate ca mene parāṃ gatāścakre ṣaṭ ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake dvādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 12 ||
aṣṭayonīmaṣṭaputrām | aṣṭapatnīmimāṃ mahīm | ahaṃ veda na me mṛtyuḥ | na cāmṛtyuraghā'harat, iti | aṣṭayonyaṣṭaputrām | aṣṭapadidamantarikṣam | ahaṃ veda na me mṛtyuḥ | na cāmṛtyuraghā'harat | aṣṭayonīmaṣṭaputrām | araṣṭapatnīmamūṃ divam  | ahaṃ veda na me mṛtyuḥ | na cāmṛtyuraghā'harat, iti | sutrāmāṇaṃ mahīmūṣu, iti | aditirdyauraditirantarikṣam | aditirmātā sa pitā sa putraḥ | viśve devā aditiḥ pañcajanāḥ | aditirjātamaditirjanitvama, iti | aṣṭau putrāso aditeḥ | ye jātāstanvaḥ pari | devāṃ3 upapraitsaptabhiḥ  | parā mārtāṇḍamāsyat, iti | saptabhiḥ putrairaditiḥ | upapraitpūrvyaṃ yugam | prajāyai mṛtyave tat | parā mārtāṇḍamābharaditi, iti | tānanukramiṣyāmaḥ, iti | mitraśca varuṇaśca | dhātā cāryamā ca | aṃśaśca bhagaśca | indraśca vivasvāṃścetyete, iti | hiraṇyagarbho haṃsaḥ śuciṣat | brahmajajñānaṃ taditpadamiti, iti | garbhaḥ prājāpatyaḥ | atha puruṣaḥ saptapuruṣaḥ , iti || amūṃ divaṃ saptabhirete catvāri ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake trayodaśo'nuvākaḥ || 13 ||
yo'sau tapannudeti | sa sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇānādāyodeti | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇānādāyodagāḥ, iti | asau yo'stameti | sa sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇānādāyāstametiṃ | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇānādāyāstaṃ gāḥ, iti | asau ya āpūryati | sa sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairāpūryati  | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairāpūriṣṭhāḥ, iti | asau yo'pakṣīyati | sa sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapakṣīyati | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapakṣeṣṭhāḥ, iti | amūni nakṣatrāṇi | sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapa prasarpanti cotsarpanti ca | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapa prasṛpata motsṛpata , iti | ime māsāścārdhamāsāśca | sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapa prasarpanti cotsarpanti ca | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapa prasṛpata motsṛpata || ima ṛtavaḥ | sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapa prasarpanti cotsarpanti ca | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapa prasṛpata motsṛpata, iti | ayam saṃvatsaraḥ | sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapa prasarpati cotsarpati ca  | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapa prasṛpa motsṛpa, iti | idamahaḥ | sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapa prasarpati cotsarpati ca | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapa prasṛpa motsṛpa | iyaṃ rātriḥ | sarveṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ prāṇairapa prasarpati cotsarpati ca | mā me prajāyā mā paśūnām | mā mama prāṇairapa prasṛm motsṛpa, iti | oṃ bhūrbhuvuḥ svaḥ, iti | etadvo mithunaṃ mā no mithunaṃ rīḍhvam,  iti | prāṇairāpūryati motsṛpata cotsarpati ca motsṛpa dve ca || udetyastametyāpūryatyapakṣīyatyamūni nakṣatrāṇīme māsā ima ṛtavo'yaṃ saṃvatsara idamahariyaṃ rātrirdaśa ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake caturdaśo'nuvākaḥ || 14 ||
athā''dityasyāṣṭapuruṣasya, iti | vasūnāmādityānām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni, iti | rudrāṇāmādityānām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni, iti | satām̐ satyānām | ādityānām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni, iti | abhidhūnvatāmabhighnatām | vātavatāṃ marutām | ādityānām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni, iti | ṛbhūṇāmādityānām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni, iti | saṃvatsarasya savituḥ | ādityasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni, iti | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ | raśmayo vo mithunaṃ mā no mithunam̐ rīḍhvam,  iti | ṛbhūṇāmādityānām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni ṣaṭ ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake pañcadaśo'nuvākaḥ || 15 || 1.15
ārogasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | bhrājasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | paṭarasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | pataṅgasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | svarṇarasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | jyotiṣīmatasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vibhāsasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | kaśyapasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ | āpo vo mithunaṃ mā no mithunam̐ rīḍhvam , iti | ārogasya daśa ||

atha vāyorekādaśapuruṣasyaikādaśastrīkasya, iti | prabhrājamānānām̐ rudrāṇām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vyavadātānām̐ rudrāṇām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vāsukivaidyutānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhrāni | rajatānām̐ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | paruṣāṇāṃ rudrāṇām̐ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | śyāmānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | kapilānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | atilohitānāśaṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | ūrdhvānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni  | avapatantānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vaidyutānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | prabhrājamānīnāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vyavadātīnāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vāsukivaidyutīnāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | rajatānāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | paruṣāṇāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | śyāmānāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | kapilānāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | atilohitīnāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhrāni | ūrdhvānāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | avapatantīnāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vaidyutīnām̐ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ | rūpāṇi vo mithunaṃ mā no mithunaṃ rīḍhvam , iti | ūrdhvānāṃ rudrāṇāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhānyatilohitīnāṃ rudrāṇīnāṃ sthāne svatejasā bhāni pañca ca ||

saptadaśo'nuvākaḥ || 17 || 1.17
athāgneraṣṭapuruṣasya, iti | agneḥ pūrvadiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | jātavedasa upadiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | sahojaso dakṣiṇadiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | ajirāprabhava upadiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | vaiśvānarasyāparadiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | naryāpasa upadiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | paṅtirādhasa udagdiśyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | visarpiṇa upadi śyasya sthāne svatejasā bhāni | oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ | diśo vo mithunaṃ mā no mithunaṃ rīḍhvam , iti || svarekaṃ ca ||
etadraśmaya āpo rūpāṇi diśaḥ pañca ||

dakṣiṇapūrvasyāṃ diśi visarpī narakaḥ | tasmānnaḥ paripāhi, iti | dakṣiṇāparasyāṃ diśyavisarpī narakaḥ | tasmānnaḥ paripāhi, iti| uttarapūrvasyāṃ diśi viṣādī narakaḥ | tasmānnaḥ paripāhi, iti | uttarāparasyāṃ diśyaviṣādī narakaḥ | tasmānnaḥ paripāhi, iti | ā yasminsapta vāsavā indriyāṇi śatakratavityete , iti || dakṣiṇapūrvasyāṃ nava ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhaka ekonaviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 19 || 1.19
indraghoṣā vo vasubhiḥ purastādupadadhatām | manojavaso vaḥ pitṛbhirdakṣiṇata upadadhatām | pracetā vo rudraiḥ paścādupadadhatām | viśvakarmā va ādityairuttarata upadadhatām | tvaṣṭā vo rūpairupariṣṭādupadadhatām | saṃjñānaṃ vaḥ paścāditi, iti | ādityaḥ sarvo'gniḥ pṛthivyām | vāyurantarikṣe | sūryo divi | candramā dikṣu | nakṣatrāṇi svaloke, iti | evā hyeva | evā hyagne | evā hi vāyo | evā hīndra | evā hi pūṣan | evā hi devāḥ , iti | dikṣu, sapta ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake viṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 20 || 1.20
āpamāpāmapaḥ sarvāḥ | asmādasmādito'mutaḥ | agnirvāyuśca sūryaśca | saha saṃcaskararddhiyā | vāyvaśvā raśmipatayaḥ | marīcyā tmāno adruhaḥ | devīrbhuvanasūvarīḥ | putravattvāya me suta | mahānāmnīrmahāmānāḥ | mahaso mahasaḥ svaḥ  devīḥ parjanyasū varīḥ | putravattvāya me suta | apāśnuṣṇimapā rakṣaḥ | apāśnuṣṇimapāragham | apāghrāmapa cāvartim | apa devīrito hita | vajraṃ devīrajītāṃśca | bhuvanaṃ devasūvarīḥ | ādityānaditiṃ devīm | yoninordhvamudīṣata  bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāmaṃ devāḥ | bhadraṃ paśyemākṣabhiryajatrāḥ | sthirairaṅgaistuṣṭuvāsaṃstanūbhiḥ | vyaśema devahitaṃ yadāyuḥ | svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ | svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ | svasti nastārkṣyo ariṣṭanemiḥ | svasti no bṛhaspatirdadhātu | ketavo aruṇāsaśca | ṛṣayo vātaraśanāḥ | pratiṣṭhāṃ śatadhā hi | samāhitāso sahasradhāyasam | śivā naḥ śaṃtamā bhavantu | divyā āpa oṣadhayaḥ | sumṛḍīkā sarasvati | mā te vyoma saṃdṛśi , iti | svarudīṣata vātaraśanāḥ ṣaṭca | |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhaka ekaviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 21 || 1.21
yo'pāṃ puṣpaṃ veda | puṣpavānprajāvānpaśumānbhavati | candramā vā apāṃ puṣpam | puṣpavānprajāvānpaśumānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | yo'pāmāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati | agnirvā apāmāyatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | yo'gnerāyatanaṃ veda  | āyatanavānbhavati | āpo vā agnerāyatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | yo'pāmāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati | vāyurvā apāmāyatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | yo vāyorāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati  | āpo vai vāyorāyatanam | āyatanānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | yo'pāmāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati | asau vai tapannapāmāyatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | yo'muṣya tapata āyatanaṃ veda | āyanavānbhavati | āpo vā amuṣya tapata āyatanam  | āyatanavānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | yo'pāmāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati | candramā vā apāmāyatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | yaścandramasa āyatanaṃ veda | āyatavānbhavati | āpo vai candramasa āyatanam | āyatanānbhavati  | ya evaṃ veda, iti | yo'pāmāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati | nakṣatrāṇi vā apāmāyatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | yo nakṣatrāṇāmāyatanaṃ veda | āyatanavānbhavati | āpo vai nakṣatrāṇāmā yatanam | āyatanavānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda , iti | yo'pāmāyatanaṃ veda | puṣkaraiśca sam̐stīrya | tasminvihāyase | agniṃ praṇīyopasamādhāya,, iti | brahmavādino vadanti | kasmātpraṇīte'yamagniścīyate | sāppraṇīte'yamapsu hyayaṃ cīyate | asau bhuvane'pyanāhitāgniretāḥ | āpo vā idamāsantsalilameva | sa prajāpatirekaḥ puṣkaraparṇe samabhavat | tasyāntarmanasi kāmaḥ samavartata | idam̐ sṛjeyamiti | tasmādyatpuruṣo manasā'bhigacchati | tadvācā vadati | tatkarmaṇā karoti | tadeṣā'bhyanūktā, iti |
kāmastadagre samavartatādhi | manaso retaḥ prathamaṃ yadāsīt  | sato bandhumasati niravindan | hṛdi pratīṣyā kavayo manīṣeti, iti | upainaṃ tadupanamati | yatkāmo bhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | sa tapo'tapyata | sa tapastaptvā | śarīramadhūnuta | tasya yanmām̐samāsīt | tato'ruṇāḥ ketavo vātaraśanā ṛṣaya udatiṣṭhan  | ye nakhāḥ | te vaikhānasāḥ | ye vālāḥ | te vālakhilyāḥ | yo rasaḥ | so'pām, iti |
antarataḥ kūrmaṃ bhūtam̐ sarpantam | tamabravīt | mama vai tvaṅmām̐sā | samabhūt | netyabravīt | pūrvamevāhamihā''samiti | tatpuruṣasya puruṣatvam | sa sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ | sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt | bhūtvodatiṣṭhat | tamabravīt | tvaṃ vai pūrvam̐ samabhūḥ | tvamidaṃ pūrvaḥ kuruṣveti, iti | sa ita ādāyāpaḥ  | añjalinā purastādupādadhāt | evā hyeveti | tata āditya udatiṣṭhat | sā prācī dik, iti | athā''ruṇaḥ keturdakṣiṇata upādadhāt | evā hyagna iti | tato vā agnirudatiṣṭhat | sā dakṣiṇā dik | athā''ruṇaḥ ketuḥ paścādupādadhāt | evā hi vāyo iti |  tato vāyurudatiṣṭhat | sā pratīcī dik | athā''ruṇaḥ keturuttarata upādadhāt | evā hīndreti | tato vā indra udatiṣṭhat | sodīcī dik | athā''ruṇaḥ keturmadhya upādadhāt | evā hi pūṣanniti | tato vai pūṣodatiṣṭhat | seyaṃ dik |  athā''ruṇaḥ keturupariṣṭādupādadhāt | evā hi devā iti | tato devamanuṣyāḥ pitaraḥ | gandharvāpsaścodatiṣṭhan | sordhvā dik, iti | yā vipruṣo vi parāpatan | tābhyo'surā rakṣāṃsi piśācāścodatiṣṭhan | tasmātte parābhavan | vipruḍbhyo hi te samabhavan, iti | tadeṣā'bhyanūktā , iti | āpo ha yadbṛhatīrgarbhamāyan | dakṣaṃ dadhānā janayantīḥ svayaṃbhum | tata ime'dhyasṛjyanta sargāḥ | adbhyo vā idaṃ samabhūt | tasmādidaṃ sarvaṃ brahma svayaṃbhviti, iti | tasmādidaṃ sarvaṃ śithilamivādhruvamivābhavat, iti | prajāpatirvāva tat | ātmanā''tmānaṃ vidhāya | tadevānuprāviśat, iti | tadeṣā'bhyanūktā  iti | vidhāya lokānvidhāya bhūtāni | vidhāya sarvāḥ pradiśo diśaśca | prajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasya | ātmanā''tmānamabhisaṃviveśeti, iti | sarvamevedamāptvā | sarvamavarudhya | tadevānupraviśati | ya evaṃ veda  iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake trayoviṃśo'nuvākaḥ | | 1.23 ||
catuṣṭayya āpo gṛhṇāti | catvāri vā apāṃ rūpāṇi | megho vidyut | stanayitnurvṛṣṭiḥ | tānyevāvarundhe, iti | ātapati varṣyāṃ gṛhṇāti | tāḥ purastādupadadhāti | etā vai brahmavarcasyā āpaḥ | mukhata eva brahmavarcasamavarundhe | tasmānmukhato brahmavarcasitaraḥ , iti | kūpyā gṛhṇāti | tā dakṣiṇata upadadhāti | etā vai tejasvanīrāpaḥ | teja evāsya dakṣiṇato dadhāti | tasmāddakṣiṇo'rdhastejasvitaraḥ,iti | sthāvarā gṛhṇāti | tāḥ paścādupadadhāti | pratiṣṭhitā vai sthāvarāḥ | paścādeva pratitiṣṭhati, iti | vahantīrgṛhṇāti | tā uttarata upadadhāti | ojasā vā etā vahantīrivodgatīriva ākūjatīriva dhāvantīḥ | oja evāsyottarato dadhāti | tasmāduttaro'rdha ojasvitaraḥ, iti | saṃbhāryā gṛhṇāti | tā madhya upadadhāti | iyaṃ vai saṃbhāryāḥ | asyāmeva pratitiṣṭhati, iti |
palvalyā gṛhṇāti | tā upariṣṭādupādadhāti  | asau vai palvalyāḥ | amuṣyāmeva pratitiṣṭhati, iti | dikṣūpadadhāti | dikṣu vā āpaḥ | annaṃ vā āpaḥ | adbhyo vā annaṃ jāyate | yadevādbhyo'nnaṃ jāyate | tadavarundhe, iti | taṃ vā etamaruṇāḥ ketavo vātaraśanā ṛṣayo'cinvan | tasmādāruṇaketukaḥ, iti | tadeṣā'bhyanūktā, iti | kaitavo aruṇāsaśca | ṛṣayo vātaraśanāḥ | pratiṣṭhāṃ śatadhā hi | samāhitāso sahasradhāyasamiti, iti | śataśaścaiva sahasraśaśca pratitiṣṭhati | ya etamagniṃ cinute | ya u cainamevaṃ veda, iti || vrahmavarcasitaro vahantīrgṛhṇāti tā upariṣṭādupādadhātyāruṇaketuko'ṣṭau ca ||
iti kṛpṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake caturviśo'nuvākaḥ | | 1.24 | |
jānudaghnīmuttaravedīṃ khātvā | apāṃ pūrayati | apāṃ sarvatvāya, iti | puṣkaraparṇaṃ rukmaṃ puruṣamityupadadhāti | tapo vai puṣkaraparṇam | satyaṃ rukmaḥ| | amṛtaṃ puruṣaḥ | etāvadvāvāsti | yāvadetat | yāvadevāsti  | tadavarundhe, iti | kūrmamupadadhāti | apāmeva medhamavarundhe | atho svargasya lokasya samaṣṭyai, iti | āpamāpāmapaḥ sarvāḥ | asmādasmādito'mutaḥ | agnirvāyuśca sūryaśca | saha saṃcaskarardhiyā iti | vāyvaśvā raśmipatayaḥ, hati | lokaṃ pṛṇa cchidraṃ pṛṇa| yāstisraḥ paramajāḥ, iti | indraghoṣā vo vasubhirevā hyeveti, iti | pañca citaya upadadhāti | pāṅkto'gniḥ| yāvānevāgniḥ | taṃ cinute, iti | lokaṃpṛṇayā dvitīyāmupadadhāti | pañcapadā vai virāṭ | tasyā vā iyaṃ pādaḥ | antarikṣaṃ pādaḥ | dyauḥ pādaḥ | diśaḥ pādaḥ | parorajāḥ pādaḥ, iti | virājyeva pratitiṣṭhati | ya etamagniṃ cinute | ya u cainamevaṃ veda , iti || asti pṛṇāntarikṣaṃ pādaḥ ṣaṭca ||

agniṃ praṇīyopasamādhāya | tamabhita etā abīṣṭakā upadadhāti | agnihotre darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ | paśubandhe cātu rmāsyeṣu | atho āhuḥ | sarveṣu yajñakratuṣviti, iti | atha ha smā''hāruṇaḥ svāyaṃbhuvaḥ | sāvitraḥ sarvo'gnirityananuṣaṅgaṃ manyāmahe | nānā vā eteṣāṃ vīryāṇi,iti | kamagniṃ cinute  | satriyamagniṃ cinvānaḥ | kamagniṃ cinute | sāvitramagniṃ cinvānaḥ | kamagniṃ cinute | nāciketamagniṃ cinvānaḥ | kamagniṃ cinute | cāturhotriyamagniṃ cinvānaḥ | kamagniṃ cinute | vaiśvasṛjamagniṃ cinvānaḥ | kamagniṃ cinute  | upānuvākyamāśumagniṃ cinvānaḥ | kamagniṃ cinute | imamāruṇaketukamagniṃ cinvāna iti, iti | vṛṣā vā agniḥ | vṛṣāṇau sam̐sphālayet | hanyetāsya yajñaḥ | tasmānnānuṣajyaḥ, iti | sottaravediṣu kratuṣu cinvīta | uttaravedyāṃ hyagniścīyate, iti | prajākāmaścinvīta  | prājāpatyo vā eṣo'gniḥ | prājāpatyāḥ prajāḥ | prajāvānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | paśukāmaścinvīta | saṃjñānaṃ vā etatpaśūnām | yadāpaḥ | paśūnāmeva saṃjñāne'gniṃ cinute | paśu mānbhavati | ya evaṃ veda , iti | vṛṣṭikāmaścinvīta | āpo vai vṛṣṭiḥ | parjanyo varṣuko bhavati | ya evaṃ veda, iti | āmayāvī cinvīta | āpo vai bheṣajam | bheṣajamevāsmai karoti | sarvamāyureti, iti | abhicaram̐ścinvīta | vajro vā āpaḥ  | vajrameva bhrātṛvyebhyaḥ praharati | stṛṇuta enam, iti | tejaskāmro yaśaskāmaḥ | brahmavarcasakāmaḥ svargakāmaścinvīta | etāvadvāvāsti | yāvadetat | yāvadevāsti | tadavarundhe, iti | tasyaitadvratam | varṣati na dhāvet  amṛtaṃ vā āpaḥ | amṛtasyānantarityai, iti | nāpsu mūtrapurīṣaṃ kuryāt | na niṣṭhīvet | na vivasanaḥ snāyāt | guhyo vā eṣo'gniḥ | etasyāgneranatidāhāya, iti | na puṣkaraparṇāni hiraṇyaṃ vā'dhitiṣṭhet | etasyāgneranabhyārohāya, iti | na kūrmasyāśnīyāt | nodakasyāghātukānyena modakāni bhavanti | aghātukā āpaḥ | ya etamagniṃ cinute | ya u cainamevaṃ veda , iti | cinute cinute prajākāmaścinvīta ya evaṃ vedā''po dhāvedaśnīyāccatvāri ca ||
iti kṛpṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake ṣaḍviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 26 || 1.26
imā nu kaṃ bhuvanā sīṣadhema | indraśca viśve ca devāḥ iti | yajñaṃ ca naḥ stanva ca prajāṃ ca | ādityairindraḥ saha sīṣadhātu, iti | ādiratyairindraḥ sagaṇo marudbhiḥ | asmākaṃ bhūtvavitā tanūnām, iti | āplavasva praplavasva | āṇḍī bhava ja mā muhuḥ | sukhādīṃ duḥkhanidhanām | pratimuñcasva svāṃ puram  iti | marīcayaḥ svāyaṃbhuvāḥ | ye śarīrāṇyakalpayan | te te dehaṃ kalpayantu | mā ca te khyā sma tīriṣat, iti | uttiṣṭhata mā svapta | agnimicchadhvaṃ bhāratāḥ | rājñaḥ somasya tṛptāsaḥ | sūryeṇa suyujoṣasaḥ iti | yuvā suvāsāḥ, iti | aṣṭācakrā navadvārā  | devānāṃ pūrayodhyā | tasyām̐ hiraṇmayaḥ kośaḥ | svargo loko jyotiṣā''vṛtaḥ, iti | yo vai tāṃ brahmaṇo veda | amṛtenā''vṛtāṃ purīm | tasmai brahma ca brahmā ca | āyuḥ kīrtiṃ prajāṃ daduḥ, iti | vibhrājamānām̐ hariṇīm | yaśasā saṃparīvṛtām | puram̐ hiraṇmayīṃ brahmā  | viveśāparājitā, iti | parāṅetyajyāmayī | parāṅetyanāśakī | iha cāmutra cānveti | vidvāndevāsurānubhayān, iti | yatkumārī mandrayate | yadyoṣidyatpativratā | ariṣṭaṃ yatkiṃca kriyate | agnistadanuvedhati, iti | aśṛtāsaḥ śṛtāsaśca  | yajvāno ye'pyayajvanaḥ | svaryanto nāpekṣante | indramagniṃ ca ye viduḥ, iti |
sikatā iva saṃyanti | raśmibhiḥ samudīritāḥ | asmāllokādamuṣmācca | ṛṣibhiradātpṛśnibhiḥ, iti | apeta vīta vi ca sarpatātaḥ | ye'tra stha purāṇā ye ca nūtanāḥ | ahobhiradbhiraktubhirvyaktam |  | yamo dadātvavasānamasmai, iti | nṛ muṇantu nṛ pātvaryaḥ | akrṛṣṭā ye ca kṛṣṭajāḥ | kumārīṣu kanīnīṣu | jāriṇīṣu ca ye hitāḥ, iti | retaḥpītā āṇḍapītāḥ | aṅgāreṣu ca ye hutāḥ | ubhayānputrapautrakān | yuve'haṃ yamarājagān, iti | śataminnu śaradaḥ, iti ado yadbrahma vilabam | pitṛṇāṃ ca yamasya ca | varuṇasyāśvinoragneḥ | marutāṃ ca vihāyasām, iti | kāmaprayavaṇaṃ me astu | sa hyevāsmi sanātanaḥ | iti nāko brahmiśravo rāyo dhanama | putrānāpo devīrihā''hitā , iti || puraṃ navadvārā brahmā ca vyaktam̐ śarado'ṣṭau ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake saptaviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 27 || 1.27
viśīrṣṇī gṛdhraśīrṣṇī ca | apeto nirṛtim̐ hathaḥ | paribādham̐ śvetakukṣam | nijaṅgham̐ śabalodaram,iti | sa tānvācyāyayā saha | agne nāśaya saṃdṛśaḥ | īrṣyāsūye bubhukṣām | manyuṃ kṛtyāṃ ca dīdhire | rathena kim̐kṣukāvatā | agne nāśaya saṃdṛśaḥ , iti | iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake'ṣṭāviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 28 || 1.28

punardve ca ||

adbhyastirodhā'jāyata | tava vaiśravaṇaḥ sadā | tiro dhehi sapatnānnaḥ | ye apo'śnanti kecana, iti | tvāṣṭrī māyāṃ vaiśravaṇaḥ | ratham̐ sahasravandhuram | puraścakram̐ sahasrāśvam | āsthāyā''yāhi no balim, iti | yasmai bhūtāni balimāvahanti | dhanaṃ gāvo hastihiṇyamaśvān  | asāma sumatau yajñiyasya | śriyaṃ bibhrato'nnamukhīṃ virājam, iti | sudarśane ca krauñce ca| maināge ca mahāgirau | satadvāṭṭāragamantā | sam̐hāryaṃ nagaraṃ tava, iti | iti mantrāḥ | kalpo'ta ūrdhvam, iti | yadi balim̐ haret | hiraṇyanābhaye vitudaye kauberāyāyaṃ baliḥ| sarvabhūtādhipataye nama iti | atha balim̐ hṛtvopatiṣṭheta, iti | kṣattraṃ kṣattraṃ vaiśravaṇaḥ | brāhmaṇā vayam̐ smaḥ | namaste astu mā mā him̐sīḥ | asmātpraviśyānnamaddhīti, iti | atha tamagnimādadhīta | yasminnetatkarma prayuñjīta, iti | tirodhā bhūḥ | tirodhā bhuvaḥ  | tirodhā svaḥ | tirodhā bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ | sarveṣāṃ lokānāmādhipatye sīdeti, iti | atha tamagnimindhati | yasminnetatkarma prayuñjīta, iti | tirodhā bhūḥ svāhā | tirodhā bhuvaḥ svāhā | tirodhā svaḥ svāhā | tirodhā bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ svāhā, iti | yasminnasya kāle sarvā āhutīrhutā bhaveyuḥ  | api brāhmaṇamukhīnāḥ | tasminnahnaḥ kāle prayuñjīta | paraḥ suptajanādvepi, iti | mā sma pramādyantamādhyāpayet | sarvārthāḥ sidhyante | ya evaṃ ved | kṣudhyannidamajānatām | sarvārthā na sidhyante, iti | yaste vighātuko bhrātā | mamāntarhṛdayre śritaḥ  | tasmā imamagrapiṇḍaṃ juhomi | sa me'rthānmā vivadhīt | mayi svāhā, iti | rājādhirājāya prasahyasāhine | namo vayaṃ vaiśravaṇāya kurmahe | sa me kāmānkāmakāmāya mahyam | kāmeśvaro vaiśravaṇo dadātu | kuberāya vaiśravaṇāya | mahārājāya namaḥ | ketavo aruṇāsaśca | ṛṣayo vātaraśanāḥ | pratiṣṭhām̐ śatadhā hi | samāhitāso sahasradhāyasam | śivā naḥ śaṃtamā bhavantu | divyā āpa oṣadhayaḥ | sumṛḍīkā sarasvati | mā te vyoma saṃdṛśi  iti || aśvānbalirbhuvo bhaveyuḥ śritaśca sapta ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhaka ekatriṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 31 || 1.31
saṃvatsarametadvrataṃ caret | dvau vā māsau, iti | niyamaḥ samāsena, iti | tasminniyamaviśeṣāḥ | triṣavaṇamudakopasparśī | caturthakālapānabhaktaḥ syāt | aharaharvā bhaikṣamaśnīyāt | audumbarībhiḥ samidbhiragniṃ paricaret | punarmā maitvindriyamityetenānuvākena | uddhṛtaparipūtābhiradbhiḥ kāryaṃ kurvīta  | asaṃcayavān | agnaye vāyave sūryāya | brahmaṇe prajāpataye | candramase nakṣatrebhyaḥ | ṛtubhyaḥ saṃvatsarāya | varuṇāyāruṇāyeti vratahomāḥ | pravargyavadādeśaḥ | aruṇāḥ kāṇḍa ṛṣayaḥ, iti | araṇye'dhīyīran | bhadraṃ karṇebhiriti dve japitvā  | mahānāmnībhirudakam̐ sam̐sparśya | tamācāryo dadyāt | śivā naḥ śaṃtametyoṣadhīrālabhate | sumṛḍīketi bhūmim | evamapavarge | dhenurdakṣiṇā | kam̐saṃ vāsaśca kṣaumam | anyadvā śuklam | yathāśakti vā | evam̐ svādhyāyadharmeṇa | araṇye'dhīyīta | tapasvī puṇyo bhavati tapasvī puṇyo bhavati , iti || kurvīta japitvā svādhyāyadharmeṇa dve ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake prathamaprapāṭhake dvātriṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 32 || 1.32
atha śāntiḥ
bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devāḥ| bhadraṃ paśyemākṣabhiryajatrāḥ | sthirairaṅgaistuṣṭuvām̐sastanūbhiḥ | vyaśema deva hitaṃ yadāyuḥ || svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ | svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ | svasti nastārkṣyo ariṣṭanemiḥ | svasti no bṛhaspatirdadhātu ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyā'raṇyake prathamaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ samāptaḥ||1||
atha kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ |
hariḥ oṃ |
namo brahmaṇe namo astvagnaye namaḥ pṛthivyai nama oṣadhībhyaḥ | namo vāce namo vācaspataye namo viṣṇave bṛhate
karomi ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
<2>
oṃ saha vai devānāṃ cāsurāṇāṃ ca yajñau pratatāvāstāṃ vayam̐ svargaṃ lokameṣyāmo vayameṣyāma iti te'surāḥ saṃnahya sahasaivā''caranbrahmacaryeṇa tapasaiva devāste'surā amuhyam̐ste na prājānam̐ste parābhavante na svargaṃ lokamāyanprasṛtena vai yajñena devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāṃyannaprasṛtenāsurānparābhāvayan, iti | prasṛto ha vai yajñopavītino yajño'prasṛto'napavītino yatkiṃca brāhmaṇo yajñopavītyadhīte yajata eva tat, iti |
tasmādyajñopavītyevādhīyīta yājayedyajeta vā yajñasya prasṛtyai iti | ajinaṃ vāso vā dakṣiṇata upavīya, iti | dakṣiṇaṃ bāhumuddharate'vedhatte savyamiti yajñopavītametadeva viparītaṃ prācīnāvītam̐ saṃvītaṃ mānuṣam || 1 ||, iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake prathamo'nuvākaḥ || 1 || 2.1
rakṣāṃsi ha vā puronuvāke tapogramatiṣṭhanta tānprajāpatirvareṇopāmantrayata tāni varamavṛṇītā''dityo no yoddhā iti tānpra jāpatirabravīdyodhayadhvamiti tasmāduttiṣṭhantaṃ ha vā tāni rakṣāṃsyādityaṃ yodhayanti yāvadastamanvagāttāni ha vā etāni rakṣāṃsi gāyatriyā'bhimantritenāmbhasā śāmyanti, iti | tadu ha vā ete brahmavādinaḥ pūrvābhimukhāḥ saṃdhyāyāṃ gāyatriyā'bhimantritā āpa ūrdhvaṃ vikṣipanti tā etā āpo vajrībhūtvā tāni rakṣāṃsi mandehāruṇe dvīpe prakṣipanti, iti | yatpradakṣiṇaṃ prakramanti tena pāpmānamavadhūnvanti, iti | udyantamastaṃ yantamādityamabhidhyāyankurvanbrāhmaṇo vidvāntsakalaṃ bhadramaśnute'sāvādityo brahmeti, iti | brahmaiva sanbrahmāpyeti ya evaṃ veda || 2 ||, iti | iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake dvitīyo'nuvākaḥ || 2 ||
yaddevā devahelanaṃ devāsaścakṛmā vayam | ādityāstasmānmā muñcatartasyartena māmita, iti | devā jīvanakāmyā yadvācā'nṛtamūdima | tasmānna iha muñcata viśve devāḥ sajoṣasaḥ, iti | ṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī ṛtena tvaṃ sarasvati | kṛtānnaḥ pāhyenaso yatkiṃcānṛtamūdima, iti | indrāgnī mitrāvaruṇau somo dhātā bṛhaspatiḥ | te no muñcantvenaso yadanyakṛtamārima, iti | sajātaśam̐sāduta jāmiśaṃsājjyāyasaḥ śaṃsāduta vā kanīyasaḥ | anādhṛṣṭaṃ devakṛtaṃ yadenastasmāttvamusmāñjātavedo mumugdhi, iti |
yadvācā yanmanasā bāhubhyāmūrubhyāmaṣṭhīvadbhyāṃ śiśnairyadanṛtaṃ cakṛmā vayam | agnirmā tasmādenaso gārhapatyaḥ pramuñcatu cakṛma yāni duṣkṛtā, iti | yena trito arṇavānnirbabhū'va yena sūrye tamaso nirmumoca | yenendro viśvā ajahādarātīstenāhaṃ jyotiṣā jyotirānaśāna ākṣi, iti | yatkusīdamapratīttaṃ mayeha yena yamasya nidhinā carāmi | etattadagne anṛṇo bhavāmi jīvanneva prati tatte dadhāmi, iti |
tanmayi mātā yadā pipeṣa yadantarikṣaṃ yadāśasātikrāmāmi trite devā divi jātā yadāpa imaṃ me varuṇa tattvā yāmi tvaṃ no agne sa tvaṃ no agne tvamagne ayāsi , iti ||
mumugdhi sapta ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ || 3 ||
yadadīvyannṛṇamahaṃ babhūvāditsanvā saṃjagara janebhyaḥ | agnirmā tasmādindraśca saṃvidānau pramuñcatām, iti | yaddhastābhyāṃ cakara kilbiṣāṇyakṣāṇāṃ vagnumupajighnamānaḥ | ugraṃpaśyā ca rāṣṭrabhṛcca tānyapsarasāvanudattāmṛṇāni, iti |
ugraṃpaśye rāṣṭrabhṛtkilbiṣāṇi yadakṣavṛttamanudattametat | nenna ṛṇānṛṇava itsamāno yamasya loke adhirajjurāya, iti | ava te hela uduttamamimaṃ me varuṇa tattvā yāmi tvaṃ no agne sa tvaṃ no agne iti | saṃkusuko vikusuko nirṛtho yaśca nisvanaḥ | te'1smadyakṣmamanāgaso dūrāddūramacīcatam, iti | niryakṣmamacīcate kṛtyāṃ nirṛtiṃ ca | tena yo'1smatsamṛcchātai tamasmai prasuvāmasi, iti | duḥśaṃsānuśaṃsābhyāṃ ghaṇenānughaṇena ca | tenānyo'1smatsamṛcchātai tamasmai prasuvāmasi, iti | saṃ varcasā payasā saṃ tanūbhiraganmahi manasā saṃ śivena | tvaṣṭā no atra vidadhātu rāyo'numārṣṭu tanvo1 yadviliṣṭam ,iti || kṛtyāṃ nirṛtiṃ ca pañca ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake caturtho'nuvākaḥ || 4 ||
āyuṣṭe viśvato dadhadayamagnirvareṇyaḥ | punaste prāṇa āyāti parā yakṣmaṃ suvāmi te, iti | āyurdā agne haviṣo juṣāṇo ghṛtapratīko ghṛtayoniredhi | ghṛtaṃ pītvā madhu cāru gavyaṃ piteva putramabhirakṣatādimam, iti | imamagna āyuṣe varcase kṛdhi tigmamojo varuṇa saṃśiśādhi | mātevāsmā adite śarma yaccha viśve devā jaradaṣṭiryathā'sat, iti | agna āyūm̐ṣi pavasa āsuvorjamiṣaṃ ca naḥ | āre bādhasva ducchunām, iti | agne pavasva svapā asme varcaḥ suvīryam | dadhadrayiṃ mayi poṣam , iti | agnirṛṣiḥ pavamānaḥ pāñcajanyaḥ purohitaḥ | tamīmahe mahāgayam, iti | agne jātānpraṇudā naḥ sapatnānpratyajātāñjātavedo nudasva | asme dīdihi sumanā ahelañcharmante syāma trivarūtha udbhau, iti | sahasā jātānpraṇudā naḥ sapatnānpratyajātāñjātavedo nudasva | adhi no brūhi sumanasyamāno vayaṃ syāma praṇudā naḥ sapatnān, iti | agne yo no'bhito jano vṛko vāro jighāṃsati | tām̐stvaṃ vṛtrahañjahi vasvasmabhyamābhara, iti | agne yo no'bhidāsati samāno yaśca niṣṭyaḥ | taṃ vayaṃ samidhaṃ kṛtvā tubhyamagne'pidadhmasi , iti | yo naḥ śapādaśapato yaśca naḥ śapataḥ śapāt | uṣāśca tasmai nimrukca sarvaṃ pāpaṃ samūhatām, iti | yo naḥ sapatno yo raṇo marto'bhidāsati devāḥ | idhmasyeva prakṣāyato mā tasyoccheṣi kiṃcana, iti | yo māṃ dveṣṭi jātavedo yaṃ cāhaṃ dveṣmi yaśca mām | sarvām̐stānagne saṃdaha yāṃścāhaṃ dveṣmi ye ca mām, iti | yo asmabhyamarātīyādyaśca no dveṣate janaḥ | nindādyo asmāndipsācca sarvāṃstānmaṣmaṣā kuru, iti | saṃśitaṃ mre brahma saṃśitaṃ vīryāṃ1 balam | saṃśitaṃ kṣattraṃ me jiṣṇu yasyāhamasmi purohitaḥ, iti | udeṣāṃ bāhū atiramudvarco atho balam | kṣiṇomi brahmaṇā'mitrānunnayāmi svām̐ 1 aham, iti | punurmanaḥ punarāyurma āgātpunaścakṣuḥ punaḥ śrotraṃ ma āgātpunaḥ prāṇaḥ punarākūtaṃ ma āgātpunaścittaṃ punarādhītaṃ ma āgāt | vaiśvānaro me'dabdhastanūpā avabādhatāṃ duritāni viśvā , iti || poṣaṃ dadhmasi purohitaścatvāri ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake pañcamo'nuvākaḥ || 5 ||
vaiśvānarāya prativedayāmo yadīnṛṇaṃ saṃgaro devatāsu | sa etānpāśānmumucanpraveda sa no muñcātu duritādavadyāt, iti | vaiśvānaraḥ pavayānnaḥ pavitrairyatsaṃgaramabhidhāvāmyāśām | anājānanmanasā yācamāno yadatraino ava tatsuvāmi, iti | amī ye subhage divi vicṛtau nāma tārake | prehāmṛtasya yacchatāmetadbaddhakamocanama, iti | vijihīrṣva lokānkṛdhi bandhānmuñcāsi baddhakam | yoneriva pracyuto garbhaḥ sarvānpatho anuṣva, iti | sa prajānanpratigṛbhṇīta vidvānprajāpatiḥ prathamajā ṛtasya | asmābhirdattaṃ jarasaḥ parastādacchinnaṃ tantumanusaṃcarema , iti | tataṃ tantumanveke anusaṃcaranti yeṣāṃ dattaṃ pitryamāyanavat | abandhveke dadataḥ prayacchāddātuṃ cecchaknavāṃsa svarga eṣām, iti | ārabhethāmanusaṃrabhethāṃ samānaṃ panthāmavatho ghṛtena | yadvāṃ pūrtaṃ pariviṣṭaṃ yadagnau tasmai gotrāyeha jāyāpatī saṃrabhethām, iti | yadantarikṣaṃ pṛthivīmuta dyāṃ yanmātaraṃ pitaraṃ vā jihiṃsima | agnirmā tasmādenaso gārhapatya unno neṣadduritā yāni cakrama, iti | bhūmirmātā'ditirno janitraṃ bhrātā'ntarikṣamabhiśasta enaḥ | dyaurnaḥ pitā pitṛyācchaṃ bhavāsi jāmimitvā mā vivitsi lokāt, iti | yatra suhārdaḥ sukṛto madante vihāya rogaṃ tanvāṃ1ṃ svāyām | aśloṇāṅgairahrutāḥ svarge tatra paśyema pitaraṃ ca putram, iti | yadannamadmyanṛtena devā dāsyannadāsyannuta vā'kariṣyan | yaddevānāṃ cakṣuṣyāgo asti yadeva kiṃca pratijagrāhamagnirmā tasmādanṛṇaṃ kṛṇotu, iti | yadannamadmi bahudhā virūpaṃ vāso hiraṇyamuta gāmajāmavim| yaddevānāṃ cakṣuṣyāgo asti yadeva kiṃca pratijagrāhamagnirmā tasmādanṛṇaṃ kṛṇotu, iti | yanmayā manasā vācā kṛtamenaḥ kadācana | sarvasmāttasmānmelito mogdhi tvaṃ hi vettha yathātatham , iti ||
carema putraṃ ṣaṭ ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ || 6 ||
vātaraśanā ha vā ṛṣayaḥ śramaṇā ūrdhvamanthino babhūvustānṛṣayo'rthamāyaṃste nilāyamacaraṃste'nupraviśuḥ kūśmāṇḍāni tāṃsteṣvanvavindañchraddhayā ca tapasā ca,iti | tānṛṣayo'bruvankathā nilāyaṃ caratheti ta ṛṣīnabruvannamo vo'stu bhagavanto'smindhāmni kena vaḥ saparyāmeti tānṛṣayo'bruvanpavitraṃ no brūta yenārepasaḥ syāmeti ta etāni sūktānyapaśyan,iti| yaddevā deva helanaṃ yadadīvyannṛṇamahaṃ babhūvā''yuṣṭe viśvato dadhadityetairājyaṃ juhuta vaiśvānarāya prativedayāma ityupatiṣṭhata yadarvācīnameno bhrūṇahatyāyāstasmānmokṣyadhva iti, iti | ta etairajuhavuste'repaso'bhavan, iti | karmādiṣvetairjuhuyātpūto devalokānsamaśnute , iti || iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake saptamo'nuvākaḥ || 7 ||
kūśmāṇḍairjuhuyādyo'pūta iva manyeta,iti | yathā steno yathā bhrūṇahaivameṣa bhavati yo'yonau retaḥ siñcati, iti | yadarvācīnameno bhrūṇahatyāyāstasmānmucyate,iti |
yāvadeno dīkṣāmupaiti dīkṣita etaiḥ satati juhoti, iti | saṃvatsaraṃ dīkṣito bhavati saṃvatsarādevā''tmānaṃ punīte,iti | māsaṃ dīkṣito bhavati yo māsaḥ sa saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarādevā''tmānaṃ punīte caturviṃśatiṃ rātrīrdīkṣito bhavati caturviṃśatirardhamāsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarādevā''tmānaṃ punīte dvādaśa rātrīrdīkṣito bhavatiṃ dvādaśa māsāḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarā devā''tmānaṃ punīte ṣaḍrātrīrdīkṣito bhavati ṣaḍvā ṛtavaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ saṃvatsarādevā''tmānaṃ punīte tisro rātrīrdīkṣito bhavati tripadā gāyatrī gāyatriyā evā''tmātaṃ punīte,iti | na māṃsamaśnīyānna striyamupeyānnoparyāsīta jugupsetānṛtāt iti | payo brāhmaṇasya vrataṃ yavāgū rājanyasyā''mikṣā vaiśyasya, iti| | atho saumye'pyadhvara etadvrataṃ brūyāt,iti | yadi manyetopadasyāmītyodanaṃ dhānāḥ saktūndhṛtamityanuvratayedātmano'nupadāsāya , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake'ṣṭamo'nuvākaḥ || 8 ||
ajānha vai pṛśnīṃstapasyamānānbrahma svayaṃbhvabhyānarṣatta ṛṣayo'bhavantadṛṣīṇāmṛṣitvaṃ tāṃ devatāmupātiṣṭhanta yajñakāmāsta etaṃ brahmayajñamapaśyantamāharantenāyajanta iti | yadṛco'dhyagīṣata tāḥ payaāhutayo devānāmabhavanyadyajūṃṣi ghṛtāhutayo yatsāmāni somāhutayo yadatharvāṅgiraso madhvāhutayo yadbrāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthā nārāśaṃsīrmedāhutayo devānāmabhavantābhiḥ kṣudhaṃ pāpmānamapāghnannapahatapāpmāno devāḥ svargaṃ lokamāyanbrahmaṇaḥ sāyujyamṛṣayo'gacchan , iti || iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake navamo'nuvākaḥ || 9 ||
pañca vā ete mahāyajñāḥ satati pratāyante satati saṃtiṣṭhante devayajñaḥ pitṛyajño bhūtayajño manuṣyayajño brahmayatra iti,iti | yadasau juhotyapi samidhaṃ taddevayajñaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate,iti | yatpitṛbhyaḥ svadhā karotyapyapastatpitṛyajñaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate,iti |
yadbhūtebhyo baliṃ harati tadbhūtayajñaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate,iti | yadbrāhmaṇebhyo'nnaṃ dadāti tanmanuṣyayajñaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate,iti | yatsvādhyāyamadhīyītaikāmapyṛcaṃ yajuḥ sāma vā tadbrahmayajñaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate, iti | yadṛco'dhīte payasaḥ kūlyā asya pitṝntsvadhā abhivahanti yadyajūṃṣi ghṛtasya kūlyā yatsāmāni soma ebhyaḥ pavate yadatharvāṅgiraso madhoḥ kūlyā yadbrāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthā nārāśaṃsīrmedasaḥ kūlyā asya pitṝnsvadhā abhivahanti,iti | yadṛco'dhīte payaāhutibhireva taddevāṃstarpayati yadyajūm̐ṣi ghṛtāhutibhiryatsāmāni somāhutibhiryadatharvāṅgiraso madhvāhutibhiryadbrāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthā nārāśaṃsīrmedāhuratibhireva taddevām̐starpayati ta enaṃ tṛptā āyuṣā tejasā varcasā śriyā yaśasā brahmavarcasenānnādyena ca tarpayanti , iti || iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake daśamo'nuvākaḥ || 10 ||
brahmayajñena yakṣyamāṇaḥ prācyāṃ diśi grāmādachadirdarśa udīcyāṃ prāgudīcyāṃ vodita āditye dakṣiṇata upavīyopaviśya hastāvavanijya trirācāmeddviḥ parimṛjya sakṛdupaspṛśya śiraścakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre hṛdayamālabhya, iti | yattrirācāmati tena ṛcaḥ prīṇāti yaddviḥ parimṛjati tena yajūṃṣi yatsakṛdupaspṛśati tena sāmāni yatsavyaṃ pāṇiṃ pādau prokṣati yacchiraścakṣuṣī nāsike śrotre hṛdayamālabhate tenātharvāṅgiraso brāhmaṇānītihāsānpurāṇāni kalpāngāthāṃ nārāśam̐sīḥ prīṇāti, iti |
darbhāṇāṃ mahadupastīryopasthaṃ kṛtvā prāṅāsīnaḥ svādhyāyamadhīyītāpāṃ vā eṣa oṣadhīnām raso yaddarbhāḥ sarasameva brahma kurute, iti | dakṣiṇottarau pāṇī pādau kṛtvā sapavitrāvomiti pratipadyata etadvai yajustrayīṃ vidyāṃ pratyeṣā vāgetatparamamakṣaram, iti | tadetadṛcā'bhyuktam , iti | ṛco akṣare parame vyomanyasmindevā adhi viśve niṣeduryastanna veda kimṛcā kariṣyati ya ittadvidusta ime samāsata iti, iti |
trīneva prāyuṅkta bhūrbhuvuḥ svarityāhaitadvai vācaḥ satyaṃ yadeva vācaḥ satyaṃ tatprāyuṅkta, iti | atha sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīṃ triranvāha paccho'rdharcaśo'navānaṃ savitā śriyaḥ prasavitā śriyamevā''pnotyatho prajñātayaiva pratipadā chandāṃsi pratipadyate , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhaka ekādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 11 ||
grāme manasā svādhyāyamadhīyīta divā naktaṃ vā iti| iti ha smā''ha śauca āhneyaḥ, iti | utāraṇye'bala uta vācota tiṣṭhannuta vrajannutā''sīna uta śayāno'dhīyītaiva svādhyāyaṃ tapasvī puṇyo bhavati ya evaṃ vidvāntsvādhyāyamadhīte,iti | namo brahmaṇe namo astvagnaye namaḥ pṛthivyai nama oṣadhībhyaḥ | namo vāce namo vācaspataye namo viṣṇave bṛhate karomi , iti | iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake dvādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 12 ||
madhyaṃdine prabalamadhīyītāsau khalu vāvaiṣa ādityo yadbrāhmaṇastasmāttarhi tekṣṇiṣṭhaṃ tapati tadeṣā'bhyuktā, iti | citraṃ devānāmudagādanīkaṃ cakṣurmitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ | āprā dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaścetiiti |
sa vā eṣa yajñaḥ sadyaḥ pratāyate sadyaḥ saṃtiṣṭhate tasya prāksāyamavabhṛthaḥ ,iti | namo brahmaṇa iti paridhānīyāṃ triranvāha,iti | apa upaspṛśya gṛhāneti tato yatkiṃca dadāti sā dakṣiṇā , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake trayodaśo'nuvākaḥ || 13 ||
tasya vā etasya yajñasya megho havirdhānaṃ vidyudagnirvarṣaṃ havistanayitnurvaṣaṭkāro yadavasphūrjati so'nuvaṣaṭkāro vāyurātmā'māvāsyā sviṣṭakṛt, iti | ya evaṃ vidvānmeghe varṣati vidyotamāne stanayatyavasphūrjati pavamāne vāyāvamāvāsyāyāṃ svādhyāyamadhīte tapa eva tattapyate tapo hi svādhyāya iti, iti | uttamaṃ nākaṃ rohatyuttamaḥ samānānāṃ bhavati yāvantaṃ ha vā imāṃ vittasya pūrṇāṃ dadatsvargaṃ lokaṃ jayati tāvantaṃ lokaṃ jayati bhūyāṃsa cākṣayyaṃ cāpa punarmṛtyuṃ jayati brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ gacchati, iti |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake caturdaśo'nuvākaḥ || 1 4 |
tasya vā etasya yajñasya dvāvanadhyāyau yadātmā'śuciryaddeśaḥ, iti | samṛddhirdaivatāni, iti | ya evaṃ vidvānmahārātra uṣasyudite vrajaṃstiṣṭhannāsīnaḥ śayāno'raṇye grāme vā yāvattarasaṃ svādhyāyamadhīte sarvām̐llokāñjayati sarvāllokānanṛṇo'nusaṃcarati tadeṣā'bhyuktā, iti | anṛṇā asminnanṛṇāḥ parasmiṃtṛtīye loke anṛṇāḥ syāma | ye devayānā uta pitṛyāṇāḥ sarvānpatho anṛṇā ākṣīyemeti, iti | agniṃ vai jātaṃ pāpmā jagrāha taṃ devā āhutībhiḥ pāpmānamapāghnannāhutīnāṃ yajñena yajñasya dakṣiṇābhirdakṣiṇānāṃ brāhmaṇena brāhmaṇasya chandobhiśchandasāṃ svādhyāyenāpahatapāpmā svādhyāyo devapavitraṃ vā etattaṃ yo'nūtsṛjatyabhāgo vāci bhavatyabhāgo nāke tadeṣā'bhyuktā, iti | yastityāja sakhividaṃ sakhāyaṃ na tasya vācyapi bhāgo asti | yadīṃ śṛṇotyalakaṃṃ śṛṇoti na hi praveda sukṛtasya panthāmiti, iti | tasmātsvādhyāyo'dhyetavyo yaṃ yaṃ kratumadhīte tena tenāsyeṣṭaṃ bhavatyagnervāyorādityasya sāyujyaṃ gacchati tadeṣā'bhyuktā, iti | ye arvāṅuta vā purāṇe vedaṃ vidvāṃsamabhito vadantyādityameva te parivadanti sarve agniṃ dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca haṃsamiti,iti | yāvatīrvai devatāstāḥ sarvā vedavidi brāhmaṇe vasanti tasmādbrāhmaṇebhyo vedavidbhyo dive dive namaskuryānnāślīlaṃ kīrtayedetā eva devatāḥ prīṇāti , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake pañcadaśo'nuvākaḥ || 15 ||
ricyata iha vā eṣa preva ricyate yo yājayati prati vā gṛhṇāti yājayitvā pratigṛhya vā'naśnantriḥ svādhyāyaṃ vedamadhīyīta, iti | trirātraṃ vā sāvitrīṃ gāyatrīmanvātirecayati,iti | varo dakṣiṇā vareṇaiva varaṃ spṛṇotyātmā hi varaḥ , iti || iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake ṣoḍaśo'nuvākaḥ || 16 ||
duhe havā eṣa chandāṃsi yo yājayati sa yena yajñakratunā yājayetso'raṇyaṃ paretya śucau deśe svādhyāyamevainamadhīyannāsīta, iti | tasyānaśanaṃ dīkṣā sthānamupasada āsanaṃ sutyā vāgjuhūrmana upabhṛddhṛtirdhruvā prāṇo haviḥ sāmādhvaryuḥ sa vā eṣa yajñaḥ prāṇadakṣiṇo'nantadakṣiṇaḥ samṛddhataraḥ , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake saptadaśo'nuvākaḥ || 17 ||
katidhā'vakīrṇī praviśati caturdhetyāhurbrahmavādino marutaḥ prāṇairindraṃ balena bṛhaspatiṃ brahmavarcasenāgnimevetareṇa sarveṇa tasyaitāṃ prāyaścittiṃ vidāṃcakāra sudevaḥ kāśyapaḥ, iti | yo brahmacāryavakiredamāvāsyāyāṃ rātryāmagniṃ praṇīyopasamādhāya dvirājyasyopaghātaṃ juhoti kāmāvakīrṇo'smyavakīrṇo'smi kāma kāmāya svāhā kāmābhidugdho'smyabhidugdho'smi kāma kāmāya svāhetyamṛtaṃ vā ājyamamṛtamevā''tmandhatte iti | hutvā prayatāñjaliḥ kavātiryaṅṅagnimabhimantrayeta,iti | saṃ māṃ''siñcantu marutaḥ samindraḥ saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ | saṃ mā'yamagniḥ siñcatvāyuṣā ca balena cā''yuṣmantaṃ karota meti, iti | prati hāsmai marutaḥ prāṇāndadhati pratīndro valaṃ prati bṛhaspatirbrahmavarcasaṃ pratyagniritaraḥ sarvaṃ sarvatanurbhūtvā sarvamāyureti, iti |
trirabhimantrayeta triṣatyā hi devāḥ,iti | yo'pūta iva manyeta sa itthaṃ juhuyāditthamabhimantrayeta punīta evā''tmānamāyurevā''tmandhatte, iti | varo dakṣiṇā vareṇaiva varam spṛṇotyātmā hi varaḥ , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake'ṣṭādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 18 ||
bhūḥ prapadye bhuvaḥ prapadye svaḥ prapadye bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ prapadye brahma prapadye brahmakośaṃ prapadye'mṛtaṃ prapadye'mṛtakośaṃ prapadye caturjālaṃ brahmakrośaṃ yaṃ mṛtyurnāvapaśyati taṃ prapadye devānprapadye devapuraṃ prapadye parīvṛto varīvṛto brahmaṇā varmaṇā'haṃ tejasā kaśyapasya,iti | yasmai namastacchiro dharmo mūrdhānaṃ brahmottarā hanuryajño'dharā viṣṇurhṛdayaṃ saṃvatsaraḥ prajananamaśvinau pūrvapādāvatrirmadhyaṃ mitrāvaruṇāvaparapādāvagniḥ pucchasya prathamaṃ kāṇḍaṃ tata indrastataḥ prajāpatirabhayaṃ caturthaṃ, iti | sa vā eṣa divyaḥ śākvaraḥ śiśumārastaṃ ha,iti | ya evaṃ vedāpa punarmṛtyuṃ jayati jayati svargaṃ lokaṃ nādhvani pramīyate nāgnau pramīyate nāpsu pramīyate nānapatyaḥ pramīyate laghvānno bhavati,iti | dhruvastvamasi dhruvasya kṣitamasi tvaṃ bhūtānāmadhipatirasi tvaṃ bhūtānāṃ śreṣṭho'si tvāṃ bhūtānyupaparyāvartante namaste namaḥ sarvaṃ te no namaḥ śiśukumārāya namaḥ , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhaka ekonaviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 19 ||
namaḥ prācyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namaḥ,iti | namo dakṣiṇāyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namo namaḥ pratīcyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namo nama udīcyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namo nama ūrdhvāyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namo namo'dharāyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namo namo'vāntarāyai diśe yāśca devatā etasyāṃ prativasantyetābhyaśca namaḥ,iti | namo gaṅgāyamunayormadhye ye vasanti te me prasannātmānaściraṃ jīvitaṃ vardhayanti namo gaṅgāyamunayormunibhyaśca namo namo gaṅgāyamunayormunibhyaśca namaḥ , iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaprapāṭhake viṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 20 ||
saha rakṣāṃsi yaddevāḥ saptadaśa yadadīvyanpañcadaśā''yuṣṭe catustriśaṃśadvaiśvānarāya ṣaḍviṃśatirvātaraśanā ha kūśmāṇḍairajānha pañca vrahmayajñena grāme madhyaṃdine tasya vai meghastasya vai dvau ricyate duhe ha katidhā'vakīrṇī bhūrnamaḥ prācyaṃ viṃśatiḥ saha vātaraśanā ha duhe ha caturviṃśaśatiḥ || namo brahmaṇe namo astvagnaye namaḥ pṛthivyai nama oṣadhībhyaḥ | namo vāce namo vācaspataye namo viṣṇave bṛhate karomi ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake dvitīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ samāptaḥ || 2 ||
atha kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhakasyā'rambhaḥ |
hariḥ oṃ |
tacchaṃ yorāvṛṇīmahe | gātuṃ yajñāya | gātuṃ yajñapataye | daivī svastirastu naḥ | svastirmānuṣebhyaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ jigātu bheṣajam | śaṃ no astu dvipade | śaṃ catuṣpade ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||

oṃ cittiḥ sruk | cittamājyam | vāgvediḥ | ādhītaṃ barhiḥ | keto agniḥ | vijñātamagniḥ | vākpatirhotā | mana upavaktā | prāṇo haviḥ | sāmādhvaryuḥ, iti | vācaspate vidhe nāman | vidhema te nāma | vidhestvamasmākaṃ nāma | vācaspatiḥ somaṃ pibatu | ā'smāsu nṛmṇaṃ dhātsvāhā , iti || adhvaryuḥ pañca ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake prathamo'nuvākaḥ || 1 ||
pṛthivī hotā | dyauradhvaryuḥ | rudro'gnīt | bṛhaspatirupavaktā, iti | vācaspate vāco vīryeṇa | saṃbhṛtatamenā''yakṣyase | yajamānāya vāryam | ā suvaskarasmai | vācaspatiḥ somaṃ pibati | jajanadindramindriyāya svāhā , iti || pṛthivī hotā daśa ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake dvitīyo'nuvākaḥ || 2 ||
agnirhotā | aśvinā'dhvaryū | tvaṣṭā'gnīt | mitra upavaktā , iti | somaḥ somasya purogāḥ | śukraḥ śukrasya purogāḥ | śrātāsta indra somāḥ | vātāperhavanaśrutaḥ svāhā , iti || agnirhotā'ṣṭau ||
iti kṛpṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ || 3 ||
sūryaṃ te cakṣuḥ | vātaṃ prāṇaḥ | dyāṃ pṛṣṭham | antarikṣamātmā | aṅgairyajñam | pṛthivīṃ śarīraiḥ, iti | vācaspate'cchidrayā vācā | acchidrayā juhvā | divi devāvṛdhaṃ hotrāmerayasva svāhā , iti || sūryaṃ te nava ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake caturtho'nuvākaḥ || 4 ||
mahāhavirhotā | satyahaviradhvaryuḥ, acyutapājā agnīt | acyutamanā upavaktā | anādhṛṣyaścāpratidhṛṣyaśca yajñasyābhigarau | ayāsya udgātā, iti | vācaspate hṛdvidhe nāman | vidhema te nāma | vidhestvamasmākaṃ nāma | vācaspatiḥ somamapāt | mā daivyastantuśchedi mā manuṣyaḥ | namo dive | namaḥ pṛthivyai svāhā , iti |
apātrīṇi ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake pañcamo'nuvākaḥ || 5 ||
vāgghotā | dīkṣā patnī | vāto'dhvaryuḥ | āpo'bhigaraḥ | mano haviḥ | tapasi juhomi, iti | bhūrbhuvaḥ suvaḥ | brahma svayaṃbhu | brahmaṇe svayaṃbhuve svāhā , iti |
vāgghotā nava ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ || 6 ||
brāhmaṇa ekahotā | sa yajñaḥ | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | yajñaśca me bhūyāt iti | agnirdvihotā | sa bhartā | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | bhartā ca me bhūyāt, iti | pṛthivī trihotā | sa pratiṣṭhā  | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | pratiṣṭhā ca me bhūyāt, iti | antarikṣaṃ caturhotā | sa viṣṭhāḥ | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | viṣṭhāśca me bhūyāt, iti | vāyuḥ pañcahotā | sa prāṇaḥ | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśū npuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | prāṇaśca me bhūyāt , iti | candramāḥ ṣaḍḍhotā | sa ṛtūnkalpayāti | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | ṛtavaśca me kalpantām, iti | annaṃ saptahotā | sa prāṇasya prāṇaḥ | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | prāṇasya ca me prāṇo bhūyāt, iti | dyauraṣṭahotā | so'nādhṛṣyaḥ  | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | anādhṛṣyaśca bhūyāsam, iti | ādityo navahotā | sa tejasvī | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | tejasvī ca bhūyāsam, iti | prajāpatirdaśahotā | sa idaṃ sarvam | sa me dadātu prajāṃ paśūnpuṣṭiṃ yaśaḥ | sarvaṃ ca me bhūyāt , iti | pratiṣṭhā prāṇaśca me bhūyādanādhṛśyaḥ sarvaṃ ca me bhūyāt || brāhmaṇo yajño'gnirbhartā pṛthivī pratiṣṭhā'ntarikṣaṃ viṣṭhā vāyuḥ prāṇaścandramā sa ṛtūnannaṃ sa prāṇasya prāṇo dyauranādhṛṣya ādityaḥ sa tejasvī prajāpatiḥ sa idaṃ sarvaṃ ca me bhūyāt ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake saptamo'nuvākaḥ || 7 | |
agniryajurbhiḥ | savitā stomaiḥ | indra ukthāmadaiḥ | mitrāvaruṇāvāśiṣā | aṅgiraso dhiṣṇiyairagnibhiḥ | marutaḥ sadohavirdhānābhyām | āpaḥ prokṣaṇībhiḥ | oṣadhayo barhiṣā | aditirvedyā | somo dīkṣayā  | tvaṣṭedhmena | viṣṇuryajñena | vasava ājyena | ādityā dakṣiṇābhiḥ | viśve devā ūrjā | pūṣā svagākāreṇa | bṛhaspatiḥ purodhayā | prajāpatirudgīthena | antarikṣaṃ pavitreṇa | vāyuḥ pātraiḥ | ahaṃ śraddhayā , iti || dīkṣayā pātrairekaṃ ca ||

senendrasya | dhenā bṛhaspateḥ | pathyā pūṣṇaḥ | vāgvāyoḥ | dīkṣā somasya | pṛthivyagneḥ | vasūnāṃ gāyatrī | rudrāṇāṃ triṣṭuk | ādityānāṃ jagatī | viṣṇoranuṣṭuk  | varuṇasya virāṭ | yajñasya paṅktiḥ | prajāpateranumatiḥ | mitrasya śraddhā | savituḥ prasūtiḥ | sūryasya marīciḥ | candramaso rohiṇī | ṛṣīṇāmarundhatī | parjanyasya vidyut | catasro diśaḥ | catasro'vāntaradiśāḥ | ahaśca rātriśca | kṛṣiśca vṛṣṭiśca | tviṣiścāpacitiśca | āpaścauṣadhayaśca | ūrk ca sūnṛtā ca devānāṃ patnayaḥ , iti | anuṣṭug diśaḥ ṣaṭ ca ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave | aśvinorbāhubhyām | pūṣṇo hastābhyāṃ pratigṛhṇāmi | rājā tvā varuṇo nayatu | devi dakṣiṇe'gnaye hiraṇyam | tenāmṛtatvamaśyām | vayo dātre | mayo mahyamastu pratigrahītre | ka idaṃ kasmā adāt | kāmaḥ kāmāya | kāmo dātā  | kāmaḥ pratigrahītā | kāmaṃ samudramāviśa | kāmena tvā pratigṛhṇāmi | kāmaitatte | eṣā te kāma dakṣiṇā | uttānastvā''ṅgīrasaḥ pratigṛhṇātu, iti | somāya vāsaḥ | rudrāya gām | varuṇāyāśvam | prajāpataye puruṣam  | manave talpam | tvaṣṭre'jām | pūṣṇe' vim | nirṛtyā aśvataragardabhau | himavato hastinam | gandharvāpsarābhyaḥ sragalaṃkaruṇe | viśvebhyo devebhyo dhānyam | vāce'nnam | brahmaṇa odanam | sumudrāyāpaḥ  | uttānāyā''ṅgīrasāyānaḥ | vaiśvānarāya ratham, iti | vaiśvānaraḥ pratnathā nākamāruhat | divaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhandamānaḥ samanmabhiḥ | sa pūrvavajjanayajjantave dhanam | samānamajmā pariyāti jāgṛviḥ, iti | rājā tvā varuṇo nayatu devi dakṣiṇe vaiśvānarāya ratham | tenāmṛtatvamaśyām | vayo dātre | mayo mahyamastu pratigrahītre  | ka idaṃ kasmā adāt | kāmaḥ kāmāya | kāmo dātā | kāmaḥ pratigrahītā | kāmaṃ samudramāviśa | kāmena tvā pratigṛhṇāmi | kāmaitatte | eṣā te kāma dakṣiṇā | uttānastvā''ṅgīrasaḥ pratigṛhṇātu , iti || dātā puruṣamāpaḥ pratigrahītre nava ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake daśamo'nuvākaḥ || 10 ||
suvarṇaṃ gharmaṃ pariveda venam | indrasyā''tmānaṃ daśadhā carantam | antaḥ samudre manasā carantam | brahmā'nvavindaddaśahotāramarṇe | antaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śāstā janānām | ekaḥ sanbahudhā vicāraḥ | śataṃ caturhotāro yatra saṃpadaṃ gacchanti devaiḥ | sa mānasīna ātmā janānām, iti | brahmendramagniṃ jagataḥ pratiṣṭhām | diva ātmānaṃ savitāraṃ bṛhaspatim | caturhotāraṃ pradiśo'nuklṛptam | vāco vīryaṃ pratiṣṭhām | diva ātmānaṃ savitāraṃ bṛhaspatim | caturhotāraṃ pradiśo'nuklṛptam | vāco vīryaṃ tapasā'nvavindat, iti | antaḥ praviṣṭaṃ kartārametam | tvaṣṭāraṃ rūpāṇi vikurvantaṃ vipaścim  | amṛtasya prāṇaṃ yajñametam | catuhoṃtṛṇāmātmānaṃ kavayo nicikyuḥ, iti | indraṃ rājānaṃ savitārametam | vāyorātmānaṃ kavayro nicikyuḥ | raśmiṃ raśmīnāṃ madhye tapantam | ṛtasya pade kavayo nipānti, iti | ya āṇḍakośe bhuvanaṃ bibharti | anirbhiṇṇaḥ sannatha lokānvicaṣṭe | yasyā''ṇḍakośaṃ śuṣmamāhuḥ prāṇamulbam | tena klṛpto'mṛtenāhamasmi, iti | suvarṇaṃ kośaṃ rajasā parīvṛtam | devānāṃ vasudhānīṃ virājam  | amṛtasya pūrṇāṃ tāmu kalāṃ vicakṣate | pādaṃ ṣaḍḍhoturna kilā''vivitse, iti | yenartavaḥ pañcadhota klṛptā | uta vā ṣaḍdhā manasota klṛptāḥ | tat ṣaḍḍhotāramṛtubhiḥ kalpamānam | ṛtasya pade kavayo iti | ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāḥ | virūpāḥ santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ | agnistāṃ agre pramumoktu devaḥ  | prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃvidānaḥ, iti | vītaṃ stuke stuke | yuvamasmāsu niyacchatam | pra pra ye grāmyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāḥ | virūpāḥ santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ | teṣāṃ saptānāmriha rantirastu | rāyaspoṣāya suprajāstvāya suvīryāya, iti | ya āraṇyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāḥ | virūpāḥ santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ | vāyustāṃ agre pramumoktu devaḥ | prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃvidānaḥ, iti | iḍāyai sṛptaṃ ghṛtavaccarācaram | devā anvavindanguhāhitam, iti | ya āraṇyāḥ paśavo viśvarūpāḥ | virūpā santo bahudhaikarūpāḥ | teṣāṃ saptānāmiha rantirastu | rāyaspoṣāya suprajrāstvāya suvīryāya , iti || ātmā janānāṃ vikurvantaṃ vipaściṃ prajānāṃ vasudhānīṃ virājaṃ carantaṃ gomatīṃ me niyacchatvekacakraṃ vyomanmāyayā deva ekarūpā aṣṭau ca ||

sahasraśīrṣā puruṣaḥ | sahasrākṣaḥ sahasrapāt | sa bhūmiṃ viśvato vṛtvā | atyatiṣṭhaddaśāṅgulam, iti | puruṣa evedaṃ sarvam | yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhavyam | utāmṛtatvasyeśānaḥ | yadannenātirohati, iti | etāvānasya mahimā | ato jyāyāṃśca pūruṣaḥ  | pādo'sya viśvā bhūtāni | tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi, iti | tripādūrdhva udaitpuruṣaḥ | pādo'syehā'bhavātpunaḥ | tato viṣvaṅvyakrāmat | sāśanānaśane abhi, iti | tasmādvirāḍajāyata | virājo adhi pūruṣaḥ | sa jāto atyaricyata | paścādbhūmimatho puraḥ , iti | yatpuruṣeṇa haviṣā | devā yajñamatanvata | vasanto asyā''sīdājyam | grīṣma idhmaḥ śaraddhaviḥ, iti | saptāsyā''sanparidhayaḥ | triḥsapta samidhaḥ kṛtāḥ | devā yadyajñaṃ tanvānāḥ | abadhnanpuruṣaṃ paśum, iti | taṃ yajñaṃ barhiṣi praukṣan | puruṣaṃ jātamagrataḥ  | tena devā ayajanta | sādhyā ṛṣayaśca ye, iti | tasmādyajñātsarvahutaḥ | saṃbhṛtaṃ pṛṣadājyam | paśūṃstāṃścakre vāyavyān | āraṇyāngrāmyāśca ye, iti | tasmādyajñātsarvahutaḥ | ṛcaḥ sāmāni jajñire | chandāṃsi jajñire tasmāt | yajustasmādajāyata , iti | tasmādaśvā ajāyanta | ye ke cobhayādataḥ | gāvo ha jajñire tasmāt | tasmājjātā ajāvayaḥ, iti | yatpuruṣaṃ vyadadhuḥ | katidhā vyakalpayan | mukhaṃ kimasya kau bāhū | kāvūrū pādāvucyete, iti | brāhmaṇo'sya mukhamāsīt | bāhū rājanyaḥ kṛtaḥ  | ūrū tadasya yadvaiśyaḥ | padbhyām śūdro ajāyata, iti | candramā manaso jātaḥ | cakṣoḥ sūryo ajāyata | mukhādindraścāgniśca | prāṇādvāyurajāyata, iti | nābhyā āsīdantarikṣam | śīrṣṇo dyauḥ samavartata | padbhyāṃ bhūmirdiśaḥ śrotrāt | tathā lokāṃ akalpayan , iti | vedāhametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam | ādityavarṇaṃ tamasastu pāre | sarvāṇi rūpāṇi vicitya dhīraḥ | nāmāni kṛtvā'bhivadanyadāste, iti | dhātā purastādyamudājahāra | śakraḥ pravidvānpradiśaścatasraḥ | tamevaṃ vidvānamṛtaṃ iha bhavati | nānyaḥ panthā ayanāya vidyate, iti | yajñena yajñamayajanta devāḥ | tāni dharmāṇi prathamānyāsan | te ha nākaṃ mahimānaḥ sacante | yatra pūrve sādhyāḥ santi devāḥ , iti | pūruṣaḥ puro'grato'jāyata kṛto'kalpayannāsandve ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake drādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 12 ||
adbhyaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ pṛthivyai rasācca | viśvakarmaṇaḥ samavartatādhi | tasya tvaṣṭā vidadhadrūpameti | tatpuruṣasya viśvamājānamagre, iti | vedāhametaṃ puruṣaṃ mahāntam | ādityavarṇaṃ tamasaḥ parastāt | tamevaṃ vidvānamṛta iha bhavati | nānyaḥ panthā vidyate'yanāya,iti | prajāpatiścarati garbhe antaḥ | ajāyamāno bahudhā vijāyate  | tasya dhīrāḥ parijānanti yonim | marīcīnāṃ padamicchanti vedhasaḥ, iti | yo devebhya ātapati | yo devānāṃ purohitaḥ | pūrvo yo devebhyo jātaḥ | namo rucāya brāhmaye, iti | rucaṃ brāhmaṃ janayantaḥ | devā agre tadabruvan | yastvaivaṃ brāhmaṇo vidyāt | tasya devā asanvaśe, iti | hrīśca te lakṣmīśca patnyau | ahorātre pārśve | nakṣatrāṇi rūpam | aśvinau vyāttam| iṣṭaṃ maniṣāṇa | amuṃ maniṣāṇa | sarvaṃ maniṣāṇa , iti || jāyate vaśe sapta ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake trayodaśo'nuvākaḥ || 13 ||
bhartā sanbhriyamāṇo bibharti | eko devo bahudhā niviṣṭaḥ | yadā bhāraṃ tandrayate sa bhartum | nidhāya bhāraṃ punarastameti, iti | tameva mṛtyumamṛtaṃ tamāhuḥ | taṃ bhartāraṃ tamu goptāramāhuḥ | sa bhṛto bhriyamāṇo bibharti | ya enaṃ veda satyena bhartum, iti | sadyojātamuta jahātyeṣaḥ | uto jarantaṃ na jahātyekam  | uto bahūnekamaharjahāra | atandro devaḥ sadameva prārthaḥ, iti | yastadveda yata ābabhūva | saṃdhāṃ ca yāṃ saṃdadhe brahmaṇaiṣaḥ | ramate tasminnuta jīrṇe śayāne | nainaṃ jahātyahassu pūrvyeṣu, iti | tvāmāpo anu sarvāścaranti jānatīḥ | vatsaṃ payasā punānāḥ | tvamagniṃ havyavāhaṃ samintse tvaṃ bhartā mātariśvā prajānām , iti | tvaṃ yajñastvamu vevāsi somaḥ | tava devā havamāyanti sarve | tvameko'si bahūnanupraviṣṭaḥ | namaste astu suhavo ma edhi, iti | namo vāmastu śṛṇutaṃ havaṃ me | prāṇāpānāvajiraṃ saṃcarantau | hvayāmi vāṃ brahmaṇā tūrtametam | yo māṃ dveṣṭi taṃ jahitaṃ yuvānā, iti prāṇāpānau saṃvidānau jahitam | amupyāsunā mā saṃgasāthām  | taṃ me devā brahmaṇā saṃvidānau | vadhāya dattaṃ tamahaṃ hanāmi, iti | asajjajāna sata ābabhūva | yaṃ yaṃ jajāna sa u gopo asya | yadā bhāraṃ tandrayate sa bhartum | parāsya bhāraṃ punarastameti, iti | tadvai tvaṃ prāṇo abhavaḥ | mahānbhogaḥ prajāpateḥ | bhujaḥ kariṣyamāṇaḥ | yaddevānprāṇayo nava, iti || ekaṃ prajānāṃ gasāthāṃ nava ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake caturdaśo'nuvākaḥ || 14 ||
hariṃ harantamanuyanti devāḥ | viśvasyeśānaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ matīnām | brahma sarūpamanu medmāgāt | ayanaṃ mā vivadhīrvikramasva, iti | mā chido mṛtyo mā vadhīḥ | mā me balaṃ
vivṛho mā pramoṣīḥ | prajāṃ mā me rīriṣa āyurugra | nṛcakṣasaṃ tvā haviṣā vidhema, iti | sadyaścakamānāya | pravepānāya mṛtyave  | prāsmā āśā aśṛṇvan | kāmenājanayanpunaḥ, iti | kāmena me kāma āgāt | hṛdayāddhṛdayaṃ mṛtyoḥ | yadamīṣāmadaḥ priyam | tadaitūpa māmabhi, iti | paraṃ mṛtyo anuperahi panthām | yaste sva itaro devayānāt | cakṣuṣmate śṛṇvate te vravīmi | mā na prajāṃ rīriṣo mota vīrān, iti | pra pūrvyaṃ manasā vandamānaḥ | nādhamāno vṛṣabhaṃ carṣaṇīnām | yaḥ prajānāmekarāṇmānuṣīṇām | mṛtyuṃ yaje prathamajāmṛtasya  iti || mṛtyave vīrām̐ścatvāri ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake pañcadaśo'nuvākaḥ || 15 ||
taraṇirviśvadarśato jyotiṣkṛdasi sūrya | viśvamābhāsi rocanam | upayāmagṛhīto'si sūryāya tvā bhrājasvata eṣa te yoniḥ sūryāya tvā bhrājasvate, iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake ṣoḍaśo'nuvākaḥ || 16 ||
āpyāyasva madintama soma viśvābhirūtibhiḥ | bhavā naḥ saprathastamaḥ, iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake saptadaśo'nuvākaḥ || 17 ||
īyuṣṭe ye pūrvatarāmapaśyanvyucchantīmuṣasaṃ martyāsaḥ | asmābhirū nu praticakṣyā'bhūdo te yanti ye aparīṣu paśyān, iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake'ṣṭādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 18 ||
jyotiṣmatīṃ tvā sādayāmi jyotiṣkṛtaṃ tvā sādayāmi jyotirvidaṃ tvā sādayāmi bhāsvatīṃ tvā sādayāmi jvalantīṃ tvā sādayāmi malmalā bhavantīṃ tvā sādayāmi dīpyamānāṃ tvā sādayāmi rocamānāṃ tvā sādayāmyajasrāṃ tvā sādayāmi bṛhajjyotiṣaṃ tvā sādayāmi bodhayantīṃ tvā sādayāmi jāgratīṃ tvā sādayāmi, iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhaka ekonaviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 19 ||
prayāsāya svāhā''yāsāya svāhā viyāsāya svāhā saṃyāsāya svāhodyāsāya svāhā'vayāsāya svāhā śuce svāhā śokāya svāhā tapyatvai svāhā tapate svāhā brahmahatyāyai svāhā sarvasmai svāhā, iti ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake viṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 20 ||
cittaṃ saṃtānena bhavaṃ yaknā rudraṃ tanimnā paśupatiṃṃ sthūlahṛdayenāgniṃ hṛdayena rudraṃ lohitena śarvaṃ matasnābhyāṃ mahādevamantaḥpārśvenauṣiṣṭhahanaṃ śiṅgīnikośyābhyām , iti || cittamekam ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhaka ekaviṃśo'nuvākaḥ || 21 ||
cittiḥ pṛthivyagniḥ sūryaṃ te cakṣurmahāhavirhotā vāgghotā brāhmaṇa ekahotā'gniryajurbhiḥ senendrasya devasya suvarṇaṃ gharmaṃ sahasraśīrṣā'dbhyo bhartā hariṃ taraṇirāpyāyasveyuṣṭe ye jyotiṣmatīṃ prayāsāya cittamekaviṃśatiḥ || 21 ||
cittiragniryajurbhirantaḥpraviṣṭaḥ prajāpatiḥ prajayā saṃvidā nastasya dhīrā jyotiṣmatīṃ tripañcāśat || 53 ||
tacchaṃ yorāvṛṇīmahe | gātuṃ yajñāya | gātuṃ yajñapataye | daivī svastirastu naḥ | svastirmānuṣebhyaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ jigātu bheṣajam | śaṃ no astu dvipade | śaṃ catuṣpade ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ samāptaḥ || 3 ||

pravargyamantrāḥ
namo vāce yā coditā yā cānuditā tasyai vāce namo namo vāce namo vācaspataye nama ṛṣibhyo mantrakṛdbhyo mantrapatibhyo mā māmṛṣayo mantrakṛto mantrapatayaḥ parā durmāhamṛṣīnmantrakṛto mantrapatīnparādām 1 vaiśvadevīṃ vācamudyāsam̐ śivāmadastāṃ juṣṭāṃ devebhyaḥ 2 śarma me dyauḥ śarma pṛthivī śarma viśvamidaṃ jagat śarma candraśca sūryaśca śarma brahmaprajāpatī 3 bhūtaṃ vadiṣye bhuvanaṃ vadiṣye tejo vadiṣye yaśo vadiṣye tapo vadiṣye brahma vadiṣye satyaṃ vadiṣye 4 tasmā ahamidamupastaraṇamupastṛṇa upastaraṇaṃ me prajāyai paśūnāṃ bhūyādupastaraṇamahaṃ prajāyai paśūnāṃ bhūyāsam 5 prāṇāpānau mṛtyormā pātaṃ prāṇāpānau mā mā hāsiṣṭam 6 madhu maniṣye madhu janiṣye madhu vakṣyāmi madhu vadiṣyāmi madhumatīṃ devebhyo vācamudyāsam̐ śuśrūṣeṇyāṃ

brahmanpravargyeṇa pra cariṣyāmaḥ | hotargharmamabhi ṣṭuhi | agnīdrauhiṇau puroḍāśāvadhi śraya | pratiprasthātarvi hara | prastotaḥ sāmāni gāya 1yajuryuktam̐ sāmabhirāktakhaṃ tvā | viśvairdevairanumataṃ marudbhiḥ | dakṣiṇābhiḥ pratataṃ pārayiṣṇum | stubho vahantu sumanasyamānam | sa no rucaṃ dhehyahṛṇīyamānaḥ | bhūrbhuvaḥ

dyubhiraktubhiḥ pari pātamasmānariṣṭebhiraśvinā saubhagebhiḥ | tanno mitro varuṇo māmahantāmaditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ 14 kayā naścitra ābhuvadūtī sadāvṛdhaḥ sakhā | kayā śaciṣṭhayā vṛtā 15 kastvā satyo madānāṃ mam̐ hiṣṭho matsadandhasaḥ | dṛḍhā cidāruje vasu 16 abhī ṣu ṇaḥ sakhīnāmavitā jaritṛṛṇām | śataṃ bhavāsyūtibhiḥ 17 vayaḥ suparṇā upasedurindraṃ priyamedhā ṛṣayo nādhamānāḥ | apa dhvāntamūrṇuhi pūrdhi cakṣurmumugdhyasmānnidhayeva baddhān 18 
śaṃ no devīrabhiṣṭaya āpo bhavantu pītaye | śaṃ yorabhi sravantu naḥ 19 īśānā vāryāṇāṃ kṣayantīścarṣaṇīnām | apo yācāmi bheṣajam 20 sumitrā na āpa oṣadhayaḥ santu durmitrāstasmai bhūyāsuryo smāndveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ 21 āpo hi ṣṭhā mayobhuvastā na ūrje dadhātana | mahe raṇāya cakṣase 22 yo vaḥ śivatamo rasastasya bhājayateha naḥ | uśatīriva mātaraḥ 23 tasmā araṅgamāma vo yasya kṣayāya jinvatha |
āpo janayathā ca naḥ 24 pṛthivī śāntā sāgninā śāntā sā me śāntā śucam̐ śamayatu 25 antarikṣam̐ śāntaṃ tadvāyunā śāntaṃ tanme śāntam̐ śucam̐ śamayatu 26 dyauḥ śāntā sādityena śāntā sā me śāntā śucam̐ śamayatu 27 pṛthivī śāntirantarikṣam̐ śāntirdyauḥ śāntirdiśaḥ śāntiravāntaradiśāḥ śāntiragniḥ śāntirvāyuḥ śāntirādityaḥ śāntiścandra māḥ śāntirnakṣatrāṇi śāntirāpaḥ śāntiroṣadhayaḥ śāntirvanaspatayaḥ śāntirgauḥ śāntirajā śāntiraśvaḥ śāntiḥ puruṣaḥ śāntirbrahma śāntirbrāhmaṇaḥ śāntiḥ śāntireva śāntiḥ śāntirme astu śāntiḥ 28 tayāham̐ śāntyā sarvaśāntyā mahyaṃ dvipade catuṣpade ca śāntiṅ karomi śāntirme astu śāntiḥ 29 eha śrīśca hrīśca dhṛtiśca tapo medhā pratiṣṭhā śraddhā satyāṃ dharmaścaitāni mottiṣṭhantamanūttiṣṭhantu mā mām̐ śrīśca hrīśca dhṛtiśca tapo medhā pratiṣṭhā śraddhā satyaṃ dharmaścaitāni mā mā hāsiṣuḥ 30 udāyuṣā svāyuṣodoṣadhīnām̐ rasenotparjanyasya śuṣmeṇodasthāmamṛtām̐ anu 31 taccakṣurdevahitaṃ purastācchukramuccarat | paśyema śaradaḥ śataṃ jīvema śaradaḥ śataṃ nandāma śaradaḥ śataṃ modāma śaradaḥ śataṃ bhavāma śaradaḥ śataṃ śṛṇavāma śaradaḥ śataṃ pra bravāma śaradaḥ śataṃ ajītāḥ syāma śaradaḥ śataṃ jyokca sūryaṃ dṛśe 32 ya udagānmahato'rṇavādvibhrājamānaḥ sarirasya madhyātsa mā vṛṣabho lohitākṣaḥ sūryo vipaścinmanasā punātu 33 brahmaṇaḥ ścotanyasi brahmaṇa āṇī stho brahmaṇa āvapanamasi dhāriteyaṃ pṛthivī brahmaṇā mahī dhāritamenena mahadantarikṣaṃ diovaṃ dādhāra pṛthivīm̐ sadevāṃ yadahaṃ veda tadahaṃ dhārayāṇi mā madvedo'dhi vi srasat 34 medhāmanīṣe māviśatām̐ samīcī bhūtasya bhavyasyāvaruddhyai sarvamāyurayāṇi sarvamāyurayāṇi 35 ābhirgīrbhiryadato na ūnamāpyāyaya harivo vardhamānaḥ | yadā stotṛbhyo mahi gotrā rujāsi bhūyiṣṭhabhājo adha te syāma 36 brahma prāvādiṣma tanno mā hāsīt | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ 37 parāvato dadhātu baddhāñ jinvatha dṛśe sapta ca  4.42

prapāṭhaka 5
pravargyabrāhmaṇa
hariḥ oṃ | śaṃ naḥ tanno mā hāsīt | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ |
devā vai satramāsata | ṛddhiparimitaṃ yaśaskāmāḥ | te'bruvan | yannaḥ prathamaṃ yaśa ṛcchāt | sarveṣāṃ nastatsahāsaditi | teṣāṃ kurukṣetraṃ vedirāsīt | tasyai khāṇḍavo dakṣiṇārdha āsīt | tūrghnamuttarārdhaḥ | parīṇaj jaghanārdhaḥ | marava utkaraḥ 1 teṣāṃ makhaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yāśa ārcchat | tannyakāmayata | tenāpākrāmat | taṃ devā anvāyan | yaśo varurutsamānāḥ | tasyānvāgatasya | savyāddhanurajāyata | dakṣiṇādiṣavaḥ | tasmādiṣudhanvaṃ puṇyajanma | yajñajanmā hi 2 tamekaṃ santam | bahavo nābhyadhṛṣṇuvan | tasmādekamiṣudhan!vinam | bahavo'niṣudhanvā nābhi dhṛṣṇuvanti | so'smayata | ekaṃ mā santaṃ bahavo nābhyadharṣiṣuriti | tasya siṣmiyāṇasya tejo'pākrāmat | taddevā oṣadhīṣu nyamṛjuḥ | te śyāmākā abhavan | smayākā vai nāmaite 3 tatsmayākānāṃ smayākatvam | tasmāddīkṣitenāpigṛhya smetavyam | tejaso dhṛtyai | sa dhanuḥ pratiṣkabhyātiṣṭhat | tā upadīkā abruvanvaraṃ vṛṇāmahai | atha va imaṃ randhayāma | yatra kva ca khanāma | tadapo'bhi tṛṇadāmeti | tasmādupadīkā yatra kva ca khananti | tadapo'bhi tṛndanti 4 vārevṛtaṃ hyāsām | tasya jyāmapyādan | tasya dhanurvipravamāṇaṃ śira udavartayat | taddyāvāpṛthivī anu prāvartata yatprāvartata | tatpravargyasya pravargyatvam | yadghrāṃ3 ityapatat | tadgharmasya gharmatvam | mahato vīryamapaptaditi | tanmahāvīrasya mahāvīratvam 5 yadasyāḥ samabharan | tatsamrājñaḥ samrāṭtvam | taṃ stṛtaṃ devatāstredhā vyagṛhṇata | agniḥ prātaḥsavanam | indro mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | viśve devāstṛtīyasavanam | tenāpaśirṣṇā yajñena yajamānāḥ | nāśiṣo'vārundhata | na suvargaṃ lokamabhyajayan | te devā aśvināvabruvan 6 bhiṣajau vai sthaḥ | idaṃ yajñasya śiraḥ prati dhattamiti | tāvabrūtāṃ varaṃ vṛṇāvahai | graha eva nāvatrāpi gṛhyatāmiti | tābhyāmetamāśvinamagṛhṇan | tāvetadyajñasya śiraḥ pratyadhattām | yatpravargyaḥ | tena saśīrṣṇā yajñena yajamānāḥ | avāśiṣo'rundhata | abhi suvargaṃ lokamajayan | yatpravargyaṃ pravṛṇakti | yajñasyaiva tacchiraḥ prati dadhāti | tena saśīrṣṇā yajñena yajamānaḥ | avāśiṣo rundhe | abhi suvargaṃ lokāṃ jayati | tasmādeṣa āśvinapravayā iva | yatpravargyaḥ
7 utkaro hyete tṛndanti mahāvīratvamabruvannajayantsapta ca
sāvitraṃ juhoti prasūtyai | caturgṛhītena juhoti | catuṣpādaḥ paśavaḥ | paśūnevāva rundhe | catasro diśaḥ | dikṣveva prati tiṣṭhati | chandāṃsi devebhyo'pākrāman | na vo bhāgāni havyaṃ vakṣyāma iti | tebhya etaccaturgṛhītamadhārayan | puronuvākyāyai yājyāyai 1 devatāyai vaṣaṭkārāya | yaccaturgṛhītaṃ juhoti | chandāṃsyeva tatprīṇāti | tānyasya prītāni devebhyo havyaṃ vahanti | brahmavādino vadanti | hotavyaṃ dīkṣitasya gṛhāi!3 na hotavyā3miti | havirvai dīkṣitaḥ | yaj juhuyāt | haviṣkṛtaṃ yajamānamagnau pra dadhyāt | yanna juhuyāt 2 yajñaparurantariyāt | yajureva vadet | na haviṣkṛtaṃ yajamānamagnau pradadhāti | na yajñaparurantareti | gāyatrī chandāṃsyatyamanyata | tasyai vaṣaṭkāro'bhyayya śiro'cchinat | tasyai dvedhā rasaḥ parāpatat | pṛthivīmardhaḥ prāviśat | paśūnardhaḥ | yaḥ pṛthivīṃ prāvi | sat 3 sa khadiro'bhavat | yaḥ paśūn | so'jām | yatkhādiryabhrirbhavati | chandasāmeva rasena yajñasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | yadaudumbarī | ūrgvā udumbaraḥ | ūrjaiva yajñasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | yadvaiṇavī | tejo vai veṇuḥ 4 tejasaiva yajñsya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | yadvaikaṅkatī | bhā evāva rundhe | devasya tvā savituḥ prasav | a ityabhrimā datte prasūtyai | aśvinorbāhubhyāmityāha | aśvinau hi devānāmadhvaryū āstām | pūṣṇo hastābhyāmityāha yatyai | vajra iva vā eṣā | yadabhriḥ | abhrirasi nārirasītyāha śāntyai 5 adhvarakṛddevebhya ityāha | yajño vā adhvaraḥ | yajñakṛddevebhya iti vāvaitadāha | uttiṣṭha brahmaṇaspata ityāha | brahmaṇaiva yajñasya śiro'cchaiti | praitu brahmaṇaspatirityāha | pretyaiva yajñasya śiro'cchaiti | pra devyetu sūnṛtetyāha | yajño vai sūnṛtā | acchā vīraṃ naryaṃ paṅktirādhasamityāha 6 pāṅkto hi yajñaḥ | devā yajñaṃ nayantu na ityāha | devāneva yajñaniyaḥ kurute | devī dyāvāpṛthivī anu me maṃsāthāmityāha ābhyāmevānumato yajñasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | ṛdhyāsamadya makhasya śira ityāha | yajño vai makhaḥ | ṛdhyāsamadya yajñasya śira iti vāvaitadāha | makhāya tvā makhasya tvā śīrṣṇa ityāha | nirdiśyaivainaddharati 7 trirharati | traya ime lokāḥ | ebhya eva lokebhyo yajñasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | tūṣṇīṃ caturthaṃ harati | aparimitādeva yajñasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | mṛtkhanādagre harati | tasmānmṛtkhanaḥ karuṇyatamaḥ | iyatyagra āsīrityāha | asyāmevācchaṃbaṭkāraṃ yajñasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | ūrjaṃ vā etaṃ rasaṃ pṛthivyā upadīkā uddihanti 8 yadvalmīkam | yadvalmīkavapā saṃbhāro bhavati | ūrjameva rasaṃ pṛthivyā ava rundhe | atho śrotrameva | śrotraṃ hyetatpṛthivyāḥ | yadvalmīkaḥ | abadhiro bhavati | ya evaṃ veda | indro vṛtrāya vajramudayacchat | sa yatrayatra parākramata 9 tannādhriyata | sa pūtīkastambe parākramata | so'dhriyata | so'bravīt | ūtiṃ vai me'dhā iti | tadūtīkānāmūtīkatvam | yadūtīkā bhavanti | yajñāyaivotiṃ dadhati | agnijā asi prajāpate reta ityāha | ya eva rasaḥ paśūnprāviśat 10 tamevāva rundhe | pañcaite saṃbhārā bhavanti | pāṅkto yajñaḥ | yāvāneva yajñaḥ | tasya śiraḥ saṃ bharati | yadgrāmyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ carmaṇā saṃbharet | grāmyānpaśūñ chucārpayet | kṛṣṇājinena saṃ bharati | āraṇyāneva paśūñ chucārpayati | tasmātsamāvatpaśūnāṃ prajāyamānānām 11 āraṇyāḥ paśavaḥ kanīyāṃsaḥ | śucā hyṛtāḥlomataḥ saṃ bharati | ato hyasya medhyam | parigṛhyā yanti | rakṣasāmapahatyai | bahavo haranti | apacitimevāsmindadhati | uddhate sikatopopte pariśrite ni dadhati śāntyai | madantībhirupa sṛjati 12 teja evāsmindadhāti | madhu tvā madhulā karotvityāha | brahmaṇaivāsmintejo dadhāti | yadgrāmyāṇaṃ pātrāṇāṃ kapālaiḥ saṃsṛjet | grāmyāṇi pātrāṇi śucārpayet | armakapālaiḥ saṃ sṛjati | etāni vā anupajīvanīyāni | tānyeva śucārpayati | śarkarābhiḥ saṃ sṛjati dhṛtyai | atho śaṃtvāya | ajalomaiḥ saṃ sṛjati | eṣā vā agneḥ priyā tanūḥ | yadajā | priyayaivainaṃ tanuvā saṃ sṛjati | athotejasā | kṛṣṇājinasya lomabhiḥ saṃ sṛjati | yajño vai kṛṣṇājinam | yajñenaiva yajñaṃ saṃ sṛjati 13 yājyāyai na juhuyādaviśdveṇuḥ śāntyai paṅktirādhasamityāha
harati dihanti parākramatāviśatprajāyamānānāṃ sṛjati śaṃtvāyāṣṭau ca
pariśrite karoti | brahmavarcasasya parigṛhītyai | na kurvannabhi prāṇyāt | yatkurvannabhiprāṇyāt | prāṇāñ chucārpayetapahāya prāṇiti | prāṇānāṃ gopīthāya | na pravargyaṃ cādityaṃ cāntareyāt | yadantareyāt | duścarmā syāt 1 tasmānnāntarāyyam | ātmano gopīthāya | veṇunā karoti | tejo vai veṇuḥ | tejaḥ pravargyaḥ | tejasaiva tejaḥ samardhayati | makhasya śiro'sītyāha | yajño vai makhaḥ | tasyaitacchiraḥ | yatpravargyaḥ 2 tasmādevamāha | yajñasya pade stha ityāha | yajñasya hyete pade | atho pratiṣṭhityai | gāyatreṇa tvā chandasā karomītyāha | chandobhirevainaṃ karoti | tr! yuddhiṃ karoti | traya ime lokāḥ | eṣāṃ lokānāmāptyai | chandobhiḥ karoti 3 vīryaṃ vai chandāṃsi | vīryeṇaivainaṃ karoti | yajuṣā bilaṃ karoti vyāvṛttyai | iyantaṃ karoti | prajāpatinā yajñamukhena saṃmitam | iyantaṃ karoti | yajñaparuṣā saṃmitam | iyantaṃ karoti | etāvadvai puruṣe vīryam | vīryasaṃmitam 4 aparimitaṃ karoti | aparimitasyāvaruddhyai | parigrīvaṃ karoti dhṛtyai | sūryasya harasā śrāyetyāha | yathāyajurevaitat | aśvaśakena dhūpayati | prājāpatyo vā aśvaḥ sayonitvāya | vṛṣṇo aśvasya niṣpadasītyāha | asau vā ādityo vṛśāśvaḥ | tasya chandāṃsi niṣpat 5 chandobhirevainaṃ dhūpayati | arciṣe tvā śociṣe tvetyāha | teja evāsmindadhāti | vāruṇo bhīddhaḥ | maitriyopaiti śāntyai | siddhyai tvetyāha | yathāyajurevaitat | devastvā savitodvapatvityāha | savitṛprasūta evainaṃ brahmaṇā devatābhirudvapati | apadyamānaḥ pṛthivyāmāśā diśa āpṛṇetyāha 6 tasmādagniḥ sarvā diśo'nu vi bhāti | uttiṣṭha bṛhanbhavordhvastiṣṭha dhruvastvamityāha pratiṣṭhityai | īśvaro vā eśo'ndho bhavitoḥ | yaḥ pravargyamanvīkṣate | sūryasya tvā cakṣuṣānvīkṣa ityāha | cakṣuṣo gopīthāya | ṛjave tvā sādhave tvā sukṣityai tvā bhūtyai tvetyāha | iyaṃ vā ṛjuḥ | antarikṣaṃ sādhu | asau sukṣitiḥ 7 diśo bhūtiḥ | imānevāsmai lokānkalpayati | atho pratiṣṭhityai | idamahamamumāmuṣyāyaṇaṃ viśā paśubhirbrahmavarcasena paryūhāmītyāha | viśaivainaṃ paśubhirbrahmavarcasena paryūhati | viśeti rājanyasya brūyāt | viśaivainaṃ paryūhati | paśubhiriti vaiśyasya | paśubhirevainaṃ paryūhati | asuryaṃ pātramanācchṛṇṇam 8 ā cchṛṇatti | devatrākaḥ | ajakṣīreṇā cchṛṇatti | paramaṃ vā etatpayaḥ | yadajakṣīram | parameṇaivainaṃ payasā cchṛṇatti | yajuṣā vyāvṛttyai | chandoghirā cchṛṇatti | chandobhirvā eṣa kriyate | chandobhireva chandāṃsyā cchṛṇatti | chṛndhi vācamityāha | vācamevāva rundhe | chṛndhyūrjamityāha | ūrjamevāva rundhe | chṛndhi havirityāha | havirevākaḥ | deva puraścara saghyāsaṃ tvetyāha | yathāyajurevaitat 9 syādyatpravargyaśchandobhiḥ karoti vīryasamitaṃ chandāṃsi niṣpatpṛṇetyāha
sukṣitiranācchṛṇṇaṃ chandāṃsyācchṛṇattyaṣṭau ca
brahmanpracariṣyāmo hotargharmamabhi ṣṭuhītyāha | eṣa vā etarhi bṛhaspatiḥ | yadbrahmā | tasmā eva pratiprocya pracarati | ātmano'nārtyai | yamāya tvā makhāya tvetyāha | etā vā etasya devatāḥ | tābhirevainaṃ samardhayati | madantībhiḥ prokṣati | teja evāsmindadhāti 1abhipūrvaṃ prokṣati | abhipūrvamevāsmintejo dadhāti | triḥ prokṣati | tr! yāvṛddhi yajñaḥ | atho medhyatvāya | hotānvāha | yajñasya śiraḥ samardhayati | prāṇāhutīrjuhoti | prāṇāneva yajamāne dadhāti | sapta juhoti | sapta vai śīrṣaṇyāḥ prāṇāḥ | prāṇānevāsmindadhāti | devastvā savitā madhvānaktvityāha 4 tejasaivainamanakti | pṛthivīṃ tapasastrāyasveti hiraṇyamupāsyati | asyā anatidāhāya | śiro vā etadyajñasya | yatpravargyaḥ | agniḥ sarvā devatāḥ | pralavānādīpyopāsyati | devatāsveva yajñasya śiraḥ prati dadhāti | apratiśīrṇāgraṃ bhavati | etadbarhirhyeṣaḥ 5 arcirasi śocirasītyāha | teja evāsminbrahmavarcasaṃ dadhāti | saṃ sīdasva mahāṃ asītyāha | mahānhyeṣaḥ | brahmavādino vadanti | ete vāva ta ṛtvijaḥ | ye darśapūrṇamāsayoḥ | atha kathā hotā yajamānāyāśiṣo nā śāsta iti | purastādāśīḥ khalu vā anyo yajñaḥ | upariṣṭādāśīranyaḥ 6 anādhṛṣyā purastāditi yadetāni yajūṃṣyāha | śīrṣata eva yajñasya yajamāna āśiṣo'va rundhe | āyuḥ purastādāha | prajāṃ dakṣiṇataḥ | prāṇaṃ paścāt | śrotramuttarataḥ | vidhṛtimupariṣṭāt | prāṇānevāsmai samīco dadhāti | īśvaro vā eṣa diśo'nūnmaditoḥ | yaṃ diśo'nu vyāsthāpayanti 7 manoraśvāsi bhūriputretīmāmabhi mṛśati | iyaṃ vai manoraśvā bhūriputrā | asyāmeva prati tiṣṭhatyanunmādāya | sūpasadā me bhūyā mā mā hiṃsīrityāhāhiṃsāyai | citaḥ stha paricita ityāha | apacitimevāsmindadhāti | śiro vā etadyajñasya | yatpravargyaḥ | asau khalu vā ādityaḥ pravargyaḥ | tasya maruto raśmayaḥ 8 svāhā marudbhiḥ pari śrayasvetyāha | amumevādityaṃ raśmibhiḥ paryūhati |
