prajāpatiṃ vai devāḥ śukraṃ payo'duhran | tadebhyo na vyabhavat | tadagnirvyakarot | tāni śukriyāṇi sāmānyabhavan | teṣāṃ yo raso'tyakṣarat | tāni śukrayajūṃṣyabhavan | śukriyāṇāṃ vā etāni śukriyāṇi | sāmapayasaṃ vā etayoranyat | devānāmanyatpayaḥ | yadgoḥ payaḥ 1 tatsāmnaḥ payaḥ | yadajāyai payaḥ | taddevānāṃ payaḥ | tasmādyatraitairyajurbhiścaranti | tatpayasā caranti | prajāpatimeva taddevānpayasānnādyena samardhayanti | eṣa ha tvai sākṣātpravargyaṃ bhakṣayati | yasyaivaṃ viduṣaḥ pravargyaḥ pravṛjyate | uttaravedyāmudvāsayettejaskāmasya | tejo vā uttaravediḥ 2 tejaḥ pravargyaḥ | tejasaiva tejaḥ samardhayati | uttaravedyāmudvāsayedannakāmasya | śiro vā etadyajñasya | yatpravargyaḥ | mukhamuttaravediḥ | śīrṣṇaiva mukhaṃ saṃ dadhātyannādyāya | annāda eva bhavati | yatra khalu vā etamudvāsitaṃ vayāṃsi paryāsate | pari vai tāṃ samāṃ prajā vayāṃsyāsate 3 tasmāduttaravedyāmevodvāsayet | prajānāṃ gopīthāya | puro vā paścādvodvāsayet | purastādvā etaj jyotirudeti | tatpaścānni mrocati | svāmevainaṃ yonimanūdvāsayati | apāṃ madhya udvāsayet | apāṃ vā etanmadhyāj jyotirajāyata | jyotiḥ pravargyaḥ | sva evainaṃ yonau prati ṣṭhāpayati 4 yaṃ dviṣyāt | yatra sa syāt | tasyāṃ diśyudvāsayet | eṣa vā agnirvaiśvānaraḥ | yatpravargyaḥ | agninaivainaṃ vaiśvānareṇābhi pra vartayati | audumbaryāṃ śākhāyāmudvāsayet | ūrgvā udumbaraḥ | annaṃ prāṇaḥ | śuggharmaḥ 5 idamahamamuṣyāmuṣyāyaṇasya śucā prāṇamapi dahāmītyāha | śucaivāsya prāṇamapi dahati | tājagārtimārcchati | yatra darbhā upadīkasantatāḥ syuḥ | tadudvāsayedvṛṣṭikāmasya | etā vā apāmanūjjhāvaryo nāma | yaddarbhāḥ | asau khalu vā āditya ito vṛṣṭimudīrayati | asāvevāsmā ādityo vṛṣṭiṃ ni yacchati | tā āpo niyatā dhanvanā yanti 6 goḥ paya uttaravedirāsate sthāpayati gharmo yanti
prajāpatiḥ saṃbhriyamāṇaḥ | samrāṭsaṃbhṛtaḥ | gharmaḥ pravṛktaḥ | mahāvīra udvāsitaḥ | asau khalu vāvaiṣa ādityaḥ | yatpravargyaḥ | sa etāni nāmānyakuruta | ya evaṃ veda | vidurenaṃ nāmnā | brahmavādino vadanti 1 yo vai vasīyāṃsaṃ yathānāmamupacarati | puṇyārtiṃ vai sa tasmai kāmayate | puṇyārtiṃ asmai kāmayante | ya evaṃ veda | tasmādevaṃ vidvān | gharma iti divā cakṣīta | saṃrāḍiti naktam | ete vā etasya priye tanuvau | ete asya priye nāmanī | priyayaivainaṃ tanuvā 2 priyeṇa nāmnā samardhayati | kīrtirasya pūrvā gacchati janatāmāyataḥ | gāyatrī devebhyo'pākrāmat | tāṃ devāḥ pravargyeṇaivānu vyabhavan | pravargyeṇāpnuvan | yaccaturviṃśatiḥ kṛtvaḥ pravargyaṃ pravṛṇakti | gāyatrīmeva tadanu vi bhavati | gāyatrīmāpnoti | pūrvāsya janaṃ yataḥ kīrtirgacchati | vaiśvadevaḥ saṃsannaḥ 3 vasavaḥ pravṛktaḥ | somo'bhikīryamāṇaḥ | āśvinaḥ payasyānīyamāne | mārutaḥ kvathan | pauṣṇa udantaḥsārasvato viṣyandamānaḥ | maitraḥ śarogṛhītaḥ | teja udyato vāyuḥ | hriyamāṇaḥ prajāpatiḥ | hūyamāno vāgghṛtaḥ 4 asau khalu vāvaiṣa ādityaḥ | yatpravargyaḥ | sa etāni nāmānyakuruta | ya evaṃ veda | vidurenaṃ nāmnā | brahmavādino vadanti | yanmṛnmayamāhutiṃ nāśnute'tha | kasmādeṣo'śnuta iti | vāgeṣa iti brūyāt | vācyeva vācaṃ dadhāti 5 tasmādaśnute | prajāpatirvā eṣa dvādaśadhā vihitaḥ | yatpravargyaḥ | yatprāgavakāśebhyaḥ | tena prajā asṛjata | avakāśairdevāsurānasṛjata | yadūrdhvamavakāśebhyaḥ | tenānnamasṛjata | annaṃ prajāpatiḥ | prajāpatirvāvaiṣaḥ
6 vadanti tanuvā saṃsanno hūyamāno vāgghuto dadhātyeṣaḥ
savitā bhūtvā prathame'hanpra vṛjyate | tena kāmāṃ eti | yaddvitīye'hanpravṛjyate | agnirbhūtvā devāneti | yattṛtīye'hanpravṛjyate | vāyurbhūtvā prāṇāneti | yaccaturthe'hanpravṛjyate | ādityo bhūtvā raśmīneti | yatpañcame hanpravṛjyate | candramā bhūtvā nakṣatrāṇyeti 1 yatṣaṣṭhe'hanpravṛjyate | ṛturbhūtvā saṃvatsarameti | yatsaptame'hanpravṛjyate | dhātā bhūtvā śakvarīmeti | yadaṣṭhame'hanpravṛjyate | bṛhaspatirbhūtvā gāyatrīmeti | yannavame'hanpravṛjyate | mitro bhūtvā trivṛta imāṃ lokāneti | yaddaśame'hanpravṛjyate | varuṇo bhūtvā virājameti 2 yadekādaśe'hanpravṛjyate | indro bhūtvā triṣṭubhameti | yaddvādaśe'hanpravṛjyate | somo bhūtvā sutyāmeti | yatpurastādupasadāṃ pravṛjyate | tasmāditaḥ parāṅ amūṃ lokāṃstapanneti | yadupariṣṭādupasadāṃ pravṛjyate | tasmādamuto'rvāṅ imāṅ lokāṃstapanneti | ya evaṃ veda | aiva tapati 3 nakṣatrāṇyeti virājameti
tapati śaṃ naḥ tanno mā hāsīt
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake pañcamaprapāṭhakaḥ samāptaḥ
<6>
atha kṛṣṇayajurvedīya taittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhakasyārambhaḥ
saṃ tvā siñcāmi yajuṣā prajāmāyurdhanaṃ ca ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ oṃ ||
pare yuvāṃsaṃ pravato mahīranu bahubhyaḥ panthāmanapaspaśānam | vaivasvataṃ saṃgamanaṃ janānāṃ yamaṃ rājānaṃ haviṣā duvasyata, iti | idaṃ tvā vastraṃ prathamaṃ nvāgan, iti | apaitadūha yadihābibhaḥ purā | iṣṭāpūrtamanusaṃpaśya dakṣiṇāṃ yathā te dattaṃ bahudhā vi bandhuṣu, iti | imau yunajmi te vahnī asunīthāya voḍhave | yābhyāṃ yamasya sādanaṃ sukṛtāṃ cāpi gacchatāt | pūṣā tvetaścyāvayatu pra vidvānanaṣṭapaśarbhuvanasya gopāḥ | saṃ tvaitebhyaḥ paridadātpitṛbhyo'gnirdevebhyaḥ suvidatrebhyaḥ | pūṣemā āśā anuveda sarvāḥ so asmāṃ abhayatamena neṣat | svastidā aghṛṇiḥ sarvavīro'prayucchanpura etu pravidvān  | āyurviśvāyuḥ paripāsati tvā pūṣā tvā pātu prapathe purastāt | yatrā''sate sukṛto yatra te yayustatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu | bhuvanasya pata idaṃ haviḥ, iti | agnaye rayimate svāhā | puruṣasya sayāvaryapedaghāni mṛjmahe | yathā no atra nāparaḥ purā jarasa āyati, iti | puruṣasya sayāvari vi te prāṇamasisrasam | śarīreṇa mahīmihi svadhayehi pitṝnupa prajayā'smānihā''vaha | maivaṃ māṃstā priye'haṃ devī satī pitṛlokaṃ yadaiṣi | viśvavārā namasā(nabhasā?) saṃvyayantyubhau no lokau payasā'bhyāvavṛtsva  | iyaṃ nārī patilokaṃ vṛṇānā nipadyata upa tvā martya pretam | viśvaṃ purāṇamanupālayantī tasmai prajāṃ draviṇaṃ ceha dhehi | udīrṣva nāryabhi jīvalokamitāsumetamupaśeṣa ehi | hastagrābhasya didhiṣostvametatpatyurjanitvamabhisaṃbabhūva | suvarṇaṃ hastādādadānā mṛtasya śriyai brahmaṇe tejase balāya | atraiva tvamiha vayaṃ suśevā viśvā spṛdho abhimātīrjayema |
dhanurhastādādadānā mṛtasya śriyai kṣattrāyaujase balāya | atraiva tvamiha vayaṃ suśevā viśvā spṛdho abhimātīrjayema | maṇiṃ hastādādadānā mṛtasya śriyai viśe puṣṭyai balāya | atraiva tvamiha vayaṃ suśevā viśvā spṛdho abhimātīrjayema  | imamagne camasaṃ mā vijīhvaraḥ priyo devānāmuta somyānām | eṣa yaścamaso devapānastasmindevā amṛtā mādayantām | agnervarma pari gobhirvyayasva saṃprorṇuṣva medasā pīvasā ca | nettvā dhṛṣṇurharasā jarhṛṣāṇo dadhadvidhakṣyanparyaṅkhayātai | mainamagne vidaho mā'bhiśoco mā'sya tvacaṃ cikṣipo mā śarīram | yadā śṛtaṃ karavo jātavedo'themenaṃ prahiṇutātpitṛbhyaḥ | śṛtaṃ yadā'karasi jātavedo'themenaṃ paridattātpitṛbhyaḥ | yadā gacchātyasunītimetāmathā devānāṃ vaśanīrbhavāti | sūryaṃ te cakṣurgacchatu vātamātmā dyāṃ ca gaccha pṛthivīṃ ca dharmaṇā | apo vā gaccha yadi tatra te hitamoṣadhīṣu pratitiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ | ajo'bhāgastapasā taṃ tapasva taṃ te śocistapatu taṃ te arciḥ | yāste śivāstanuvo jātavedastābhirvahemaṃ sukṛtāṃ yatra lokāḥ | ayaṃ vai tvamasmādadhi tvametadayaṃ vai tadasya yonirasi | vaiśvānaraḥ putraḥ pitre lokakṛjjātavedo vahemaṃ sukṛtāṃ yatra lokāḥ  || vidvānabhyāvavṛtsvābhimātīrjayema śarīraiścatvāri ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake prathamo'nuvākaḥ || 1 ||
ya etasya patho goptārastebhyaḥ svāhā ya etasya patho rakṣitārastebhyaḥ svāhā ya etasya patho'bhi rakṣitārastebhyaḥ svāhā khyātre svāhā'pākhyātre svāhā'bhilālapate svāhā'palālapate svāhā'gnaye karmakṛte svāhā yamatra nādhīmastasmai svāhā, iti |
yasta idhmaṃ jabharatsiṣvidāno mūrdhānaṃ vā tatapate tvāyā | divo viśvasmātsīmaghāyata uruṣyaḥ, iti | asmāttvamadhi jāto'si tvadayaṃ jāyatāṃ punaḥ | agnaye vaiśvānarāya suvargāya lokāya svāhā , iti | ya etasya tvatpañca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake dvitīyo'nuvākaḥ || 2 | |
pra ketunā bṛhatā bhātyagnirāvirviśvāni vṛṣabho roravīti | divaścidantādupa māmudānaḍapāmupasye mahiṣo vavardha, iti | idaṃ ta ekaṃ para ū ta ekaṃ tṛtīyena jyotiṣā saṃviśasva | saṃveśanastanuvai cāruredhi priyo devānāṃ parame sadhasthe, iti |
nāke suparṇamupa yatpatantaṃ hṛdā venanto abhyacakṣata tvā | hiraṇyapakṣaṃ varuṇasya dūtaṃ yamasya yonau śakunaṃ bhuraṇyum, iti | atidrava sārameyau śvānau caturakṣau śabalau sādhunā pathā | athā pitṝntsuvidatrāṃ apīhi yamena ye sadhamādaṃ madanti, iti | yau te śvānau yama rakṣitārau caturakṣau pathirakṣī nṛcakṣasā | tābhyāṃ rājanparidehyenaṃ svasti cāsmā anamīvaṃ ca dhehi , iti | uruṇasāvasutṛpāvuluṃbalau yamasya dūtī carato'vaśāṃ anu | tāvasmabhyaṃ dṛśaye sūryāya punardattāvasumadyeha bhadram, iti |
soma ekebhyaḥ pavate ghṛtameka upāsate | yebhyo madhu pradhāvati tāṃścidevāpi gacchatāt, iti | ye yudhyante pradhaneṣu śūrāso ye tanutyajaḥ | ye vā sahasradakṣiṇāstāṃścidevāpi gacchatāt, iti | tapasā ye anādhṛṣyāstapasā ye suvargatāḥ | tapo ye cakrire mahattāṃścidevāpi gacchatāt, iti | aśmanvatī revatīḥ saṃrabhadhvamuttiṣṭhata prataratā sakhāyaḥ | atrā jahāma ye asannaśevāḥ śivānvayamabhi vājānuttarema , iti | yadvai devasya savituḥ pavitraṃ sahasradhāraṃ vitatamantarikṣe | yenāpunādindramanārtamārtyai tenāhaṃ māṃ sarvatanuṃ punāmi | yā rāṣṭrātpannādapayanti śākhā abhimṛtā nṛpatimicchamānāḥ | dhātustāḥ sarvāḥ pavanena pūtāḥ prajayā'smānrayyā varcasā saṃsṛjātha | udvayaṃ tamasasparipaśyanto jyotiruttaram | devaṃ devatrā sūryamaganma jyotiruttamam, iti | dhātā punātu savitā punātu | agnestejasā sūryasya varcasā  || dhehyuttaremāṣṭau ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ || 3 ||
yaṃ te agnimamanthāma vṛṣabhāyeva paktave | imaṃ taṃ śamayāmasi kṣīreṇa codakena ca, iti | yaṃ tvamagne samadahastvamu nirvāpayā punaḥ | kyāmbūratra jāyatāṃ pākadūrvāvyalkaśā, iti | śītike śītikāvati hrāduke hrādukāvati | maṇḍūkyāsu saṃgamayemaṃ svagniṃ śamaya, iti | śaṃ te dhanvanyā āpaḥ śamu te santvanūkyāḥ | śaṃ te samudriyā āpaḥ śamu te santu varṣyāḥ, iti | śaṃ te sravantīstanuve śamu te santu kūpyāḥ | śaṃ te nīhāro varṣatu śamu pṛṣvā'vaśīyatām , iti | avasṛja punaragne pitṛbhyo yasta āhutaścarati svadhābhiḥ | āyurvasāna upayātu śeṣaṃ saṃgacchatāṃ tanuvā jātavedaḥ, iti | saṃgacchasva pitṛbhiḥ saṃ svadhābhiḥ samiṣṭāpūrtena parame vyoman | yatra bhūmyai vṛṇase tatra gaccha tatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu, iti | yatte kṛṣṇaḥ śakuna ātutoda pipīlaḥ sarpa uta vā śvāpadaḥ | agniṣṭadviśvādanṛṇaṃ kṛṇotu somaśca yo brāhmaṇamāviveśa, iti | uttiṣṭhātastanuvaṃ saṃbharasva meha gātramavahā mā śarīram | yatra bhūmyai vṛṇase tatra gaccha tatra tvā devaḥ savitā dadhātu | idaṃ ta ekaṃ para ū ta ekaṃ tṛtīyena jyotiṣā saṃviśasva | saṃveśanastunavai cāruredhi priyo devānāṃ parame sadhasthe, iti | uttiṣṭha prehi pradravaukaḥ kṛṇuṣva parame vyoman | yamena tvaṃ yamyā saṃvidānottamaṃ nākamadhirohemam | aśmanvatī revatīryadvai devasya savituḥ pavitraṃ yā rāṣṭrātpannādudvayaṃ tamasaspari dhātā punātu, iti| asmāttvamadhijāto'syayaṃ tvadadhijāyatām | agnaye vaiśvānarāya suvargāya lokāya svāhā , iti || avaśīyatāṃ sadhasthe pañca ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭaprapāṭhake caturtho'nuvākaḥ || 4 ||
āyātu devaḥ sumanābhirūtibhiryamo ha veha prayatābhiraktā | āsīdatāṃ suprayate ha barhiṣyūrjāya jātyai mama śatruhatyai | yame iva yatamāne yadaitaṃ pra vāṃ bharanmānuṣā devayantaḥ | āsīdataṃ svamu lokaṃ vidāne svāsasthe bhavatamindave naḥ, iti |
yamāya somaṃ sunuta yamāya juhutā haviḥ | yamaṃ ha yajño gacchatyagnidūto araṃkṛtaḥ | yamāya ghṛtavaddhavirjuhota pra ca tiṣṭhata | sa no deveṣvāyamaddīrghamāyuḥ prajīvase | yamāya madhumattamaṃ rājñe havyaṃ juhotana | idaṃ nama ṛṣibhyaḥ pūrvajebhyaḥ pūrvebhyaḥ pathikṛdbhyaḥ  | yo'sya kauṣṭhya jagataḥ pārthivasyaika idvaśī | yamaṃ bhaṅgyaśravo gāya yo rājā'naparodhyaḥ, iti | yamaṃ gāya bhaṅgyaśravo yo rājā'naparodhyaḥ | yenā''po nadyo dhanvāni yena dyauḥ pṛthivī dṛḍhā, iti | hiraṇyakakṣyāntsudhurānhiraṇyākṣānayaḥśaphān | aśvānanaśyato dānaṃ yamo rājā'bhitiṣṭhati, iti | yamo dādhāra pṛthivīṃ yamo viśvamidaṃ jagat | yamāya sarvamitrasthe yatprāṇadvāyurakṣitam, iti | yathā pañca yathā ṣaḍyathā pañcadaśarṣayaḥ | yamaṃ yo vidyātsa brūyādyathaika ṛṣirvijānate , iti | trikadrukebhiḥ patati ṣaḍurvīrekamidbṛhat | gāyatrī triṣṭupchandāṃsi sarvā tā yama āhitā, iti | aharaharnayamāno gāmaśvaṃ puruṣaṃ jagat | vaivasvato na tṛpyati pañcabhirmānavairyamaḥ, iti | vaivasvate vivicyante yame rājani te janāḥ | ye ceha satyenecchante ya u cānṛtavādinaḥ, iti | te rājanniha vivicyante'thā yanti tvāmupa | devāṃśca ye namasyanti brāhmaṇāṃścāpacityati, iti | yasminvṛkṣe supalāśe devaiḥ saṃpibate yamaḥ | atrā no viśpatiḥ pitā purāṇā anuvenati , iti || pathikṛdbhyo vijānate'nuvenati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake pañcamo'nuvākaḥ || 5 ||
vaiśvānare haviridaṃ juhomi sāhasramutsaṃ śatadhārametam | tasminneṣa pitaraṃ pitāmahaṃ prapitāmahaṃ bibharatpinvamāne, iti | drapsaścaskanda pṛthivīmanu dyāmimaṃ ca yonimanu yaśca pūrvaḥ | tṛtīyaṃ yonimanu saṃcarantaṃ drapsaṃ juhomyanu sapta hotrāḥ, iti | imaṃ samudraṃ śatadhāramutsaṃ vyacyamānaṃ bhuvanasya madhye | ghṛtaṃ duhānāmaditiṃ janāyāgne mā hiṃsīḥ parame vyoman, iti | apeta vīta vi ca sarpatāto ye'tra stha purāṇā ye ca nūtanāḥ | ahobhiradbhiraktubhirvyaktaṃ yamo dadātvavasānamasmai, iti | savitaitāni śarīrāṇi pṛthivyai māturupastha ādadhe | tebhiryujyantāmaghniyāḥ, iti | śunaṃ vāhāḥ śunaṃ nārāḥ śunaṃ kṛṣatu lāṅgalam | śunaṃ varatrā badhyaṃntāṃ śunamaṣṭrāmudiṅgaya śunāsīrā śunamasmāsu dhattam, iti | śunāsīrāvimāṃ vācaṃ yaddivi cakrathuḥ payaḥ | tenemāmupasiñcatam, iti | sīte vandāmahe tvā'rvācī subhage bhava | yayā naḥ subhagā sasi yathā naḥ suphalā sasi, iti | savitaitāni śarīrāṇi pṛthivyai māturupastha ādadhe | tebhiradite śaṃ bhava, iti | vimucyadhvamaghniyā devayānā atāriṣma tamasaspāṃramasya | jyotirāpāma suvaraganma , iti | pra vātā vānti patayanti vidyuta udoṣadhīrjiṃhate pinvate suvaḥ | irā viśvasmai bhuvanāya jāyate yatparjanyaḥ pṛthivīṃ retasā'vati, iti | yathā yamāya hārmyamavapanpañca mānavāḥ | evaṃ vapāmi hārmyaṃ yathā'sāma jīvaloke bhūrayaḥ, iti | citaḥ stha paricita ūrdhvacitaḥ śrayadhvaṃ pitaro devatā | prajāpatirvaḥ sādayatu tayā devatayā, iti| āpyāyasva, saṃ te , iti ||
aghniyā aganma sapta ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ || 6 ||
utte tabhnomi pṛthivīṃ tvatparīmaṃ lokaṃ nidadhanmo ahaṃ riṣam | etāṃ sthūṇāṃ pitaro dhāraṃyantu te'trā yamaḥ sādanātte minotu, iti | upasarpa mātaraṃ bhūmimetāmuruvyacasaṃ pṛthivīṃ suśevām | ūrṇamradā yuvatirdakṣiṇāvatyeṣā tvā pātu nirṛtyā upasthe, iti | ucchmañcasva pṛthivi mā vibādhithāḥ sūpāyanā'smai bhava sūpavañcanā | mātā putraṃ yathā sicā'bhyenaṃ bhūmi vṛṇu. iti | ucchramañcamānā pṛthivī hi tiṣṭhasi sahasraṃ mita upa hi śrayantām | te gṛhāso madhuścuto viśvāhā'smai śaraṇāḥ santvatra, iti | eṇīrdhānā hariṇīrarjunīḥ santu dhenavaḥ | tilavatsā ūrjamasmai duhānā viśvāhā santvanapasphurantīḥ , iti | eṣā te yamasādane svadhā nidhīyate gṛhe | akṣitirnāma te asau, iti | idaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ prabharema barhirdevebhyo jīvanta uttaraṃ bharema | tattvamārohāso medhyo bhavaṃ(bhava?) yamena tvaṃ yamyā saṃvidānaḥ, iti | mā tvā vṛkṣau saṃbādhiṣṭāṃ mā mātā pṛthivi tvam | pitṝnhyatra gacchāsyedhāṃsaṃ yamarājye, iti | mā tvā vṛkṣau saṃbādhethāṃ mā mātā pṛthivī mahī | vaivasvataṃ hi gacchāsi yamarājye virājasi, iti | nalaṃ plavamārohaitaṃ nalena patho'nvihi | sa tvaṃ nalaplavo bhūtvā saṃtara pratarottara , iti | savitaitāni śarīrāṇi pṛthivyai māturupastha ādadhe | tebhyaḥ pṛthivi śaṃ bhava, iti | ṣaḍḍhotā sūryaṃ te cakṣurgacchatu vātamātmā dyāṃ ca gaccha pṛthivīṃ ca dharmaṇā | apo vā gaccha yadi tatra te hitamoṣadhīṣu pratitiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ, iti | paraṃ mṛtyo anuparehi panthāṃ yaste sva itaro devayānāt | cakṣuṣmate śṛṇvate te bravīmi mā naḥ prajāṃ rīriṣo mota vīrān, iti | śaṃ vātaḥ śaṃ hi te ghṛṇiḥ śamu te santvoṣadhīḥ | kalpantāṃ me diśaḥ śagmāḥ, iti | pṛthivyāstvā loke sādayāmyamuṣya śarmāsi pitaro devatā | prajāpatistvā sādayatu tayā devatayā, iti | antarikṣasya tvā divastvā diśāṃ tvā nākasya tvā pṛṣṭhe bradhnasya tvā viṣṭape sādayāmyamuṣya śarmāsi pitaro devatā | prajāpatistvā sādayatu tayā devatayā , iti ||
anapasphurantīruttara devatayā dve ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake saptamo'nuvākaḥ || 7 ||
apūpavānghṛtavāṃścarureha sīdatūttabhnuvanpṛthivīṃ dyāmutopari | yonikṛtaḥ pathikṛtaḥ saparyata ye devānāṃ ghṛtabhāgā iha stha | eṣā te yamasādane svadhā nidhīyate gṛhe'sau | daśākṣarā tāṃ rakṣasva tāṃ gopāyasva tāṃ te paridadāmi tasyāṃ tvā mā dabhanpitaro devatā | prajāpatistvā sādayatu tayā devatayā, iti | apūpavāñśṛtavānkṣīravāndadhivānmadhumāṃścarureha sīdatūttabhnuvanpṛthivīṃ dyāmutopari| yoni kṛtaḥ pathikṛtaḥ saparyata ye devānāṃ śṛtabhāgāḥ kṣaḥibhāgā dadhibhāgābhāgā iha stha| eṣa te yamusādane svadhā nidhīyate gṛhe'sau | śatākṣarā sahasrākṣarā'yutākṣarā'cyutākṣarā tāṃ rakṣasva tāṃ gopāyasva tāṃ te paridadāmi tasyāṃ tvāmādabhanpitaro devatā | prajāpatistvā sādayatu tayā devatayā , iti ||
apūpavānasau daśa ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake'ṣṭamo'nuvākaḥ || 8 ||
etāste svadhā amṛtāḥ karomi yāste dhānāḥ parikirāmyatra | tāste yamaḥ pitṛbhiḥ saṃvidāno'tra dhenūḥ kāmadughāḥ karotu, iti | tvāmarjunauṣadhīnāṃ payo brahmāṇa idviduḥ | tāsāṃ tvā madhyādādade carubhyo apidhātave, iti | dūrvāṇāṃ stambamāharaitāṃ priyatamāṃ mama | imāṃ diśaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhūyiṣṭhā'nu virohatu iti | kāśānāṃ stambamāhara rakṣasāmapahatyai | ya etasyai diśaḥ parābhavannaghāyavo yathā te nābhavānpunaḥ, iti | darbhāṇāṃ stambamāhara pitṛṇāmoṣadhīṃ priyām | anvasyai mūlaṃ jīvādanu kāṇḍamatho phalam, iti | lokaṃ pṛṇa tā asya sūdadohasaḥ iti | śaṃ vātaḥ śaṃ hi te ghṛṇiḥ śamu te santvoṣadhīḥ | kalpantāṃ te diśaḥ sarvāḥ, iti | idameva meto'parāmārtimārāma kāṃcana | tathā tadaśvibhyāṃ kṛtaṃ mitreṇa varuṇena ca, iti | varaṇo vārayādidaṃ devo vanaspatiḥ | ārtyai nirṛtyai dveṣācca vanaspatiḥ, iti | vidhṛtirasi vidhārayāsmadaghā dveṣāṃsi, iti | śami śamayāsmadaghā dveṣāṃsi, iti | yava yavayāsmadaghā dveṣāṃsi, iti | pṛthivīṃ gacchāntarikṣaṃ gaccha divaṃ gaccha diśo gaccha suvargaccha suvargaccha diśo gaccha divaṃ gacchāntarikṣaṃ gaccha pṛthivīṃ gacchāpo vā gaccha yadi tatra te hitamoṣadhīṣu pratitiṣṭhā śarīraiḥ, iti | aśmanvatī revatīryadvai devasya savituḥ pavitraṃ yā rāṣṭrātpannādudvayaṃ tamasaspari dhātā punātu, iti || atho phalaṃ punātu ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake navamo'nuvākaḥ || 9 ||
ārohatā''yurjarasaṃ gṛṇānā anupūrvaṃ yatamānā yatiṣṭa | iha tvaṣṭā sujanimā suratno dīrghamāyuḥ karatu jīvase vaḥ, iti | yathā'hānyanupūrvaṃ bhavanti yathartava ṛtubhiryanti klṛptāḥ | yathā na pūrvamaparo jahātyevā dhātarāyūṃṣi kalpayaiṣām, iti | na hi te agne tanuvai krūraṃ cakāra martyaḥ | kapirbabhasti tejanaṃ punarjarāyu gauriva | apa naḥ śośucadaghamagne śuśudhyā rayim | apa naḥ śośucadaghaṃ mṛtyave svāhā | anaḍvāhamanvārabhāmahe svastaye | sa na indra iva devebhyo vahniḥ saṃpāraṇo bhava , iti | ime jīvā vi mṛtairāvavartinnabhūdbhadrā devahūtiṃ no adya | prāñjo'gāmā nṛtaye hasāya draghīya āyuḥ pratarāṃ dadhānāḥ, iti | mṛtyoḥ padaṃ yopayanto yadaima drāghīya āyuḥ
pratarāṃ dadhānāḥ | āpyāyamānāḥ prajayā dhanena śuddhāḥ pūtā bhavatha yajñiyāsaḥ, iti | imaṃ jīvebhyaḥ paridhiṃ dadhāmi mā no'nugāda paro ardhametam | śataṃ jīvantu śaradaḥ purūcīstiro mṛtyuṃ dadmahe parvatena, iti | imā nārīravidhavāḥ supatnīrāñjanena sarpiṣā samṛśantām | anaśravo anamīvā suśevā ārohantu janayo yonimagre, iti | yadāñjanaṃ traikakudaṃ jātaṃ himavataspari | tenāmṛtasya mūlenārātīrjambhayāmasi, iti | yathā tvamudbhinatsyoṣadhe pṛthivyā adhi | evamima udbhindantu kīrtyā yaśasā brahmavarcasena, iti | ajo'syajāsmadaghā dveṣāṃsi, iti | yavo'si yavayāsmadaghā dveṣāṃsi , iti ||
saṃpāraṇo bhava jambhayāmasi trīṇi ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhake daśamo'nuvākaḥ || 10 ||
apa naḥ śośucadaghamagne śuśudhyā rayim | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | sukṣetriyā sugātuyā vasūyā ca yajāmahe | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | pra yadbhandiṣṭha eṣāṃ prā''smākāsaśca sūrayaḥ | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | pra yadagneḥ sahasvato viśvato yanti sūrayaḥ | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | pra yatte agne sūrayo jāyemahi pra te vayam | apa naḥ śośucadagham , iti | tvaṃ hi viśvatomukha viśvataḥ paribhūrasi | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | dviṣo no viśvato mukhā'ti nāveva pāraya | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | sa naḥ sindhumiva nāvayā'tiparṣā svastaye | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | āpaḥ pravaṇādiva yatīrapāsmatsyandatāmagham | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | udvanādudakānīvāpāsmatsyandatāmagham | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | ānandāya pramodāya punarāgāṃ svāngṛhān | apa naḥ śośucadagham, iti | na vai tatra pramīyate gauraśvaḥ puruṣaḥ paśuḥ | yatredaṃ brahma kriyate paridhirjīvanāya kamapa naḥ śośucadagham ,iti ||
aghamaghaṃ catvāri ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaprapāṭhaka ekādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 11 ||
apaśyāma yuvatimācarantīṃ mṛtāya jīvāṃ pariṇīyamānām | andhena yā tamasā prāvṛtā'si prācīmavācīmavayannariṣṭyai, iti | mayaitāṃ māṃstāṃ bhriyamāṇā devī satī pitṛlokaṃ yadaiṣi | viśvavārā nabhasā saṃvyayantyubhau no lokau payasā''vṛṇīhi, iti | rayiṣṭhāmagniṃ madhumantamūrmiṇamūrjaḥ santaṃ tvā payasopasaṃsadema | saṃ rayyā samu varcasā sacasvā naḥ svastaye, iti | ye jīvā ye ca mṛtā ye jātā ye ca jantyāḥ |
tebhyo ghṛtasya dhārayituṃ madhudhārā vyundatī, iti | mātā rudrāṇāṃ duhitā vasūnāṃ svasā''dityānāmamṛtasya nābhiḥ | pra ṇu vocaṃ cikituṣe janāya mā gāmanāgāmaditiṃ vadhiṣṭa, iti | pibatūdakaṃ tṛṇānyattu | omutsṛjata  iti ||
vadhiṣṭa dve ca ||

iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake ṣaṣṭhaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ samāptaḥ || 6 ||

hariḥ oṃ |
śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ | śaṃ no bhavatvaryamā | śaṃ na indro bṛhaspatiḥ | śaṃ no viṣṇururukramaḥ, iti | namo brahmaṇe | namaste vāyo | tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahmāsi | tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahma vadiṣyāmi | ṛtaṃ vadi ṣyāmi | satyaṃ vadiṣyāmi | tanmāmavantu | tadvaktāramavatu | avatu mām | avatu vaktāram, iti |
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ  iti |

oṃ śīkṣāṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | varṇaḥ svaraḥ | mātrā balam | sāma saṃtānaḥ | ityuktaḥ śīkṣādhyāyaḥ, iti | śīkṣāṃ pañca |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake dvitīyo'nuvākaḥ
saha nau yaśaḥ | saha nau brahmavarcasam, iti | athātaḥ sam̐hitāyā upaniṣadaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | pañcasvadhikaraṇeṣu | adhilokamadhijyautiṣamadhividyamadhipraja madhyātmam | tā mahāsaṃhitā ityācakṣate, iti | athādhilokam | pṛthivī pūrvarūpam | dyauruttararūpam | ākāśaḥ saṃdhiḥ  | vāyuḥ saṃdhānam | ityadhilokam, iti |
athādhijyautiṣam | agniḥ pūrvarūpam | āditya uttararūpam | āpaḥ saṃdhiḥ | vaidyutaḥ saṃdhānam | ityadhijyautiṣam, iti |
athādhividyam | ācāryaḥ pūrvarūpam  | antevāsyuttararūpam | vidyā saṃdhiḥ | pravacanaṃ saṃdhānam | ityadhividyam, iti | athādhiprajam | mātā pūrvarūpam | pitottararūpam | prajā saṃdhiḥ | prajananaṃśa saṃdhānam | ityadhiprajam , iti |
athādhyātmam | adharā hanuḥ pūrvarūpam | uttarā hanuruttararūpam | vāksaṃdhiḥ | jihvā saṃdhānam | ityadhyātmam, iti | itīmā mahāsaṃhitāḥ, iti | ya evametā mahāsaṃhitā vyākhyātā veda | saṃdhīyate prajayā paśubhiḥ | brahmavarcasenānnādyena suvargyeṇa lokena , iti || sadhirācāryaḥ pūrvarūpamityadhiprajaṃ lokena || iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ ||3||
yaśchandasāmṛṣabho viśvarūpaḥ | chandobhyo'dhyamṛtātsaṃbabhūva | sa mendro medhayā spṛṇotu | amṛtasya deva dhāraṇo bhūyāsam, iti | śarīraṃ me vicarṣaṇam | jihvā me madhumattamā | karṇābhyāṃ bhūri viśruvam | brahmaṇaḥ kośo'si medhayā'pihitaḥ | śrutaṃ me gopāya, iti |
āvahantī vitanvānā  | kurvāṇā cīramrātmanaḥ | vāsāṃsi mama gāvaśca | annapāne ca sarvadā | tato me śriyamāvaha | lomaśāṃ paśubhiḥ saha svāhā, iti | ā mā yantu brahmacāriṇaḥ svāhā | vi mā''yantu brahmacāriṇaḥ svāhā | pra mā''yantu brahmacāriṇaḥ svāhā | damāyantu brahmacāriṇaḥ svāhā | śamāyantu brahmacāriṇaḥ svāhā , iti | yaśo jane'sāni svāhā | śreyānvasyaso'sāni svāhā, iti | taṃ tvā bhaga praviśāni svāhā | sa mā bhaga praviśa svāhā | tasmintsahasraśākhe | ni bhagāhaṃ tvayi mṛje svāhā, iti | yathā''paḥ pravatā''yanti | yathā māsā aharjaram | evaṃ māṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ | dhātarāyantu sarvataḥ svāhā, iti | prativeśo'si pra mā bhāhi pra mā padyasva, iti | vitanvānā śamāyantu brahmacāriṇaḥ svāhā dhātarāyantu sarvataḥ svāhaikaṃ ca | |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake caturtho'nuvākaḥ || 4 ||
bhūrbhuvaḥ suvariti vā etāstisro vyāhṛtayaḥ, iti | tāsāmu ha smaitāṃ caturthīm | māhācamasyaḥ pravedayate | maha iti, iti | tadbrahma | sa ātmā | aṅgānyanyā devatāḥ, iti | bhūriti vā ayaṃ lokaḥ | bhuva ityantarikṣam | suvarityasau lokaḥ  | maha ityādityaḥ | ādityena vāva sarve lokā mahīyante, iti | bhūriti vā agniḥ | bhuva iti vāyuḥ | suvarityādityaḥ | maha iti candramāḥ| candramasā vāva sarvāṇi jyotīṃṣi mahīyante| bhūriti vā ṛcaḥ | bhuva iti sāmāni | suvariti yajūṃṣi  | maha iti brahma | brahmaṇā vāva sarve vedā mahīyante, iti | bhūriti vai prāṇaḥ | bhuva ityapānaḥ | suvariti vyānaḥ | maha ityannam | annena vāva sarve prāṇā mahīyante, iti | tā vā etāścatasraścaturdhā | catasraścatasro vyāhṛtayaḥ, iti | tā yo veda | sa veda brahma | sarve'smai devā balimāvahanti, iti | asau loko yajūṃṣi veda dve ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake pañcamo'nuvākaḥ || 5 ||
sa ya eṣo'ntarhṛdaya ākāśaḥ | tasminnayaṃ puruṣo manomayaḥ | amṛto hiraṇmayaḥ, iti | antareṇa tāluke | ya eṣa stana ivāvalambate | sendrayoniḥ | yatrāsau keśānto vivartate | vyapohya śīrṣakapāle, iti | bhūrityagnau pratitiṣṭhati | bhuva iti vāyau  | suvarityāditye | maha iti brahmaṇi | āpnoti svārājyam | āpnoti manasaspatim | vākpatiścakṣuṣpatiḥ | śrotrapatirvijñānapatiḥ | etattato bhavati | ākāśaśarīraṃ brahma | satyātma prāṇārāmaṃ mana ānandam | śāntisamṛddhamamṛtam, iti | iti prācīnayogyopāssva , iti | vāyāvamṛtamekaṃ ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ || 6 ||
pṛthivyantarikṣaṃ dyaurdiśo'vāntaradiśāḥ | agnirvāyurādityaścandramā nakṣatrāṇi | āpa oṣadhayo vanaspataya ākāśa ātmā | ityadhibhūtam, iti | athādhyātmam | prāṇo vyāno'pāna udānaḥ samānaḥ | cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ mano vāktvak | carma māṃsaṃ snāvāsthi majjā, iti | etadadhividhāya ṛṣiravocat | pāṅktaṃ vā idaṃ sarvam | pāṅktenaiva pāṅktaṃ spṛṇotīti , iti | sarvamekaṃ ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake saptamo'nuvākaḥ || 7 ||
omiti brahma, iti | omitīdaṃ sarvam, iti | omityetadanukṛti ha sma vā apyo śrāvayatyāśrāvayanti | omiti sāmāni gāyanti | oṃ śomiti śastrāṇi śam̐santi | omityadhvaryuḥ pratigaraṃ pratigṛṇāti | omiti brahma prasauti | omityagnihotramanujānāti | omiti brāhmaṇaḥ pravakṣyannāha,iti |
brahmopāpnavānīti | brahmaivopāpnoti  iti | oṃ daśa ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake'ṣṭamo'nuvākaḥ || 8 ||
ṛtaṃ ca svādhyāyapravacane ca | satyaṃ ca svādhyāyapravacane ca | tapaśca svādhyāyapravacane ca | damaśca svādhyāyapravacane ca | śamaśca svādhyāyapravacane ca | agnayaśca svādhyāyapravacane ca | agnihotraṃ ca svādhyāyapravacane ca| atithayaśca svādhyāyapravacane ca | mānuṣaṃ ca svādhyāyapravacane ca | prajā ca svādhyāyapravacane ca | prajanaśca svādhyāyapravacane ca | prajātiśca svādhyāyapravacane ca, iti | satyamiti satyavacā rāthītaraḥ | tapa iti taponityaḥ pauruśiṣṭiḥ | svādhyāyapravacane evaiti nāko maudgalyaḥ | taddhi tapastaddhi tapaḥ  iti | prajā ca svādhyāyapravacane ca ṣaṭ ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake navamo'nuvākaḥ || 9 ||
ahaṃ vṛkṣasya rerivā | kīrtiḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ gireriva | ūrdhvapavitro vājinīva svamṛtamasmi | draviṇaṃ savarcasam | sumedhā amṛtokṣitaḥ | iti triśaṅkorvedānuvacanam , iti | ahaṃ ṣaṭ ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake daśamo'nuvākaḥ || 10 ||
satyaṃ vada | dharmaṃ cara, iti | svādhyāyānmā pramadaḥ | ācāryāya priyaṃ dhanamāhṛtya prajātantuṃ mā vyavacchetsīḥ | satyānna pramaditavyam | dharmānna pramaditavyam | kuśalānna pramaditavyam | bhūtyai na pramaditavyam | svādhyāyapravacanābhyāṃ na pramaditavyam  | devapitṛkāryābhyāṃ na pramaditavyam, iti | mātṛdevo bhava | pitṛdevo bhava | ācāryadevo bhava | atithidevo bhava, iti | yānyanavadyāni karmāṇi | tāni sevitavyāni | no itarāṇi, iti | yānyasmākaṃ sucaritāni | tāni tvayopāsyāni  | no itarāṇi, iti | ye ke cāsmacchreyāṃso brāhmaṇāḥ | teṣāṃ tvayā''sanena praśvasitavyam, iti | śraddhayā deyam | aśraddhayā'deyam | śriyā deyam | hriyā deyam | bhiyā deyam | saṃvidā deyam, iti | atha yadi te karmavicikitsā vā vṛttavicikitsā brā syāt  | ye tatra brāhmaṇāḥ saṃmarśinaḥ | yuktā āyuktāḥ | alūkṣā dharmakāmāḥ syuḥ | yathā te tatra varteran | tathā tatra vartethāḥ, iti | athābhyākhyāteṣu | ye tatra brāhmaṇāḥ saṃmarśinaḥ | yuktā āyuktāḥ | alūkṣā dharmakāmaḥ syuḥ | yathā te teṣu varteran | tathā teṣu vartethāḥ, iti | eṣa ādeśaḥ | eṣa upadeśaḥ | eṣā vedopaniṣat | etadanruśāsanam | evamupāsitavyam | evamu caitadupāsyam , iti | svādhyāyapravacanābhyāṃ na pramaditavyaṃ tāni tvayopāsyāni syātteṣu varterantsapta ca ||
iti kṛpṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhaka ekādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 11 ||
śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ | śaṃ no bhavatvaryamā | śaṃ na indro bṛhaspatiḥ | śaṃ no viṣṇururukramaḥ | namo brahmaṇe | namaste vāyo | tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahmāsi | tvāmeva pratyakṣaṃ brahmāvādiṣam | ṛtamavādiṣam | satyamavādiṣam | tanmāmāvīt | tadvaktāramāvīt | āvīnmām | āvīdvaktāram | oṃ śāntiḥ śānti śāntiḥ,iti| satyamavādiṣaṃ pañca ca ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaprapāṭhake dvādaśo'nuvākaḥ || 12 ||
śaṃ naḥ śīkṣāṃ saha nau yaśchandasāṃ bhūḥ sa yaḥ pṛthivyomityṛtaṃ cāhaṃ vedamanūcya śaṃ no dvādaśa || 12 ||
śaṃ no maha ityādityo no itarāṇi trayovim̐śatiḥ || 23 || śaṃ no mitraḥ śaṃ varuṇaḥ || śaṃ no bhavatvaryamā | śaṃ na indro bṛhaspatiḥ | śaṃ no viṣṇururukramaḥ | namo brahmaṇe | namaste vāyo | tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahmāsi | tvameva pratyakṣaṃ brahma vadiṣyāmi | ṛtaṃ vadiṣyāmi | satyaṃ vadiṣyāmi | tanmāmavatu | tadvaktāramavatu | avatu mām | avatu vaktāram |
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ ||
hariḥ oṃ |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake saptamaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ samāptaḥ || 7 ||

hariḥ oṃ |
saha nāvavatu | saha nau bhunaktu | saha vīryaṃ karavāvahai | tejasvi nāvadhītamastu mā vidviṣāvahai | oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ iti |
oṃ brahmavidāpnoti param, iti | tadeṣā'bhyuktā | satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma | yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ parame vyoman | so'śnute sarvānkāmāntsaha | brahmaṇā vipaściteti, iti | tasmādvā etasmādātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ | ākāśādvāyuḥ | vāyoragniḥ | agnerāpaḥ | adbhyaḥ pṛthivī | pṛthivyā oṣadhayaḥ | oṣadhībhyo'nnam | annātpuruṣaḥ, iti | sa vā eṣa puruṣo'nnarasamayaḥ, iti | tasyedameva śiraḥ | ayaṃ dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | ayamu ttaraḥ pakṣaḥ | ayamātmā | idaṃ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake prathamo'nuvākaḥ || 1 ||
annādvai prajāḥ prajāyante | yāḥ kāśca pṛthivīṃ śritāḥ | atho annenaiva jīvanti | athainadapiyantyantataḥ | annaṃ hi bhūtānāṃ jyeṣṭham | tasmātsarvauṣadhamucyate | sarvaṃ vai te'nnamāpnuvanti | ye'nnaṃ brahmopāsate | annaṃ hi bhūtānāṃ jyeṣṭham | tasmā
tsarvauṣadhamucyate | annādbhūtāni jāyante | jātānyannena vardhante | adyate'tti ca bhūtāni | tasmādannaṃ taducyata iti, iti | tasmādvā etasmādannarasamayāt | anyo'ntara ātmā prāṇamayaḥ | tenaiṣa pūrṇaḥ, iti | sa vā eṣa puruṣavidha eva | tasya puruṣavidhatām | anvayaṃ puruṣavidhaḥ | tasya prāṇa eva śiraḥ | vyāno dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | apāna uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ | ākāśa ātmā | pṛthivī pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake dvitīyo'nuvākaḥ || 2 ||
atha tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ |
prāṇaṃ devā anuprāṇanti | manuṣyāḥ paśavaśca ye | prāṇo hi śrutānāmāyuḥ | tasmātsarvāyuṣamucyate | sarvameva ta āyuryanti | ye prāṇaṃ brahmopāsate | prāṇo hi bhūtānāmāyuḥ | tasmātsarvāyuṣamucyata iti, iti | tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā | yaḥ pūrvasya, iti | tasmādvā etasmātprāṇamayāt | anyo'ntara ātmā manomayaḥ | tenaiṣa pūrṇaḥ, iti | sa vā eṣa puruṣavidha eva | tasya puruṣavidhatām | anvayaṃ puruṣavidhaḥ | tasya yajureva śiraḥ | ṛgdakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | sāmottaraḥ pakṣaḥ | ādeśa ātmā | atharvāṅgirasaḥ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ || 3 ||
atha caturtho'nuvākaḥ |
yato vāco nivartante | aprāpya manasā saha | ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān | na bibheti kadācaneti, iti | tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā | yaḥ pūrvasya, iti | tasmādvā etasmānmanomayāt | anyo'ntara ātmā vijñānamayaḥ | tenaiṣa pūrṇaḥ, iti | sa vā eṣa puruṣavidha eva | tasya puruṣavidhatām | | anvayaṃ puruṣavidhaḥ | tasya śraddhaiva śiraḥ | ṛtaṃ dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | satyamuttaraḥ pakṣaḥ | yoga ātmā | mahaḥ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake caturtho'nuvākaḥ || 4 ||
atha pañcamo'nuvākaḥ
vijñānaṃ yajñaṃ tanute | karmāṇi tanute'pi ca | vijñānaṃ devāḥ sarve | brahma jyeṣṭhamupāsate | vijñānaṃ brahma cedveda| tasmāccenna pramādyati | śarīre pāpmano hitvā | sarvānkāmāntsamaśnuta iti, iti | tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā | yaḥ pūrvasya, iti | tasmādvā etasmādvijñānamayāt | anyo'ntara ātmā''nandamayaḥ | tenaiṣa pūrṇaḥ | sa vā eṣa puruṣavidha eva | tasya puruṣavidha tām | anvayaṃ puruṣavidhaḥ | tasya priyameva śiraḥ | modo dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | pramoda uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ | ānanda ātmā | brahma pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛpṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake pañcamo'nuvākaḥ || 5 ||
athāṣṭame ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ |
asanneva sa bhavati | asadbrahmeti veda cet | asti brahmeti cedveda | santamenaṃ tato viduriti, iti | tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā | yaḥ pūrvasya, iti | athāto'nupraśnāḥ iti | utāvidvānamuṃ lokaṃ pretya | kaścana gacchatī3 | āho vidvānamuṃ lokaṃ pretya | kaścitsamaśnutā3 u, iti | so'kāmayata | bahu syāṃ prajāyeyeti | sa tapo'tapyata | sa tapastaptvā | idaṃ sarvamasṛjata, iti | annādvai prajāḥ prajāyante | yāḥ kāśca pṛthivīṃ śritāḥ | atho annenaiva jīvanti | athainadapiyantyantataḥ | annaṃ hi bhūtānāṃ jyeṣṭham | tasmātsarvauṣadhamucyate | sarvaṃ vai te'nnamāpnuvanti | ye'nnaṃ brahmopāsate | annaṃ hi bhūtānāṃ jyeṣṭham | tasmātsarvauṣadhamucyate | annādbhūtāni jāyante | jātānyannena vardhante | adyate'tti ca bhūtāni | tasmādannaṃ taducyata iti, iti | tasmādvā etasmādannarasamayāt | anyo'ntara ātmā prāṇamayaḥ | tenaiṣa pūrṇaḥ, iti | sa vā eṣa puruṣavidha eva | tasya puruṣavidhatām | anvayaṃ puruṣavidhaḥ | tasya prāṇa eva śiraḥ | vyāno dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | apāna uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ | ākāśa ātmā | pṛthivī pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake dvitīyo'nuvākaḥ || 2 ||
atha tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ |
prāṇaṃ devā anuprāṇanti | manuṣyāḥ paśavaśca ye | prāṇo hi bhūtānāmāyuḥ | tasmātsarvāyuṣamucyate | sarvameva ta āyuryanti | ye prāṇaṃ brahmopāsate | prāṇo hi bhūtānāmāyuḥ | tasmātsarvāyuṣamucyata iti, iti | tasyaiṣa eva śārīra ātmā | yaḥ pūrvasya, iti | tasmādvā etasmātprāṇamayāt | anyo'ntara ātmā manomayaḥ | tenaiṣa pūrṇaḥ, iti | sa vā eṣa puruṣavidha eva | tasya puruṣavidhatām | anvayaṃ puruṣavidhaḥ | tasya yajureva śiraḥ | ṛgdakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | sāmottaraḥ pakṣaḥ | ādeśa ātmā | atharvāṅgirasaḥ pucchaṃ pratiṣṭhā, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake tṛtīyo'nuvākaḥ || 3 ||
atha caturtho'nuvākaḥ |
yato vāco nivartante | aprāpya manasā saha | ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān | na bibheti kadācaneti, iti | yadidaṃ kiṃca | tatsṛṣṭvā | tadevānuprāviśat, iti | tadanupraviśya | sacca tyaccābhavat | niruktaṃ cāniruktaṃ ca | nilayanaṃ cānilayanaṃ ca | vijñānaṃ cāvijñānaṃ ca | satyaṃ cānṛtaṃ ca satyamabhavat | yadidaṃ kiṃca | tatsatyamityācakṣate |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake ṣaṣṭho'nuvākaḥ || 6 ||
atha saptamo'nuvākaḥ
asadvā idamagra āsīt | tato vai sadajāyata | tadātmānaṃ svayamakuruta | tasmāttatsukṛtamucyata iti | yadvaitatsukṛtam | raso vai saḥ | rasaṃ hyevāyaṃ labdhvā''nandī bhavati | ko hyevānyātkaḥ prāṇyāt | yadeṣa ākāśa ānando na syāt | eṣa hyevā''nandayāti | yadā hyevaiṣa etasminnadṛśye'nātmye'nirukte'nilayane'bhayaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ vindate | atha so'bhayaṃ gato bhavati | yadā hyevaiṣa etasminnudaramantaraṃ kurute | aya tasya bhayaṃ bhavati | tattveva bhayaṃ viduṣo'manvānasya iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake saptamo'nuvākaḥ || 7 ||
athāṣṭamonuvākaḥ
bhīṣā'smādvātaḥ pavate | bhīṣodeti sūryaḥ | bhīṣā'svādagniścendraśca | mṛtyurdhāvati pañcama iti | saiṣā''nandasya mīmāṃsā bhavati, iti | yuvā syātsādhuyuvā'dhyāyakaḥ| āśiṣṭho dṛḍhiṣṭho baliṣṭhaḥ | tasyeyaṃ pṛthivī sarvā vittasya pūrṇā syāt| sa eko mānuṣa ānandaḥ, iti | te ye śataṃ mānuṣā ānandāḥ  | sa eko manuṣyagandharvāṇāmānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ manuṣyagandharvāṇāmānandāḥ | sa eko devagandharvāṇāmānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ devagandharvāṇāmānandāḥ | sa ekaḥ pitṛṇāṃ ciralokalokānāmānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ pitṛṇāṃ ciralokalokānāmānandāḥ | sa eka ājānajānāṃ devānāmānandaḥ |  | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śatamājānajānāṃ devānāmānandāḥ | sa ekaḥ karmadevānāṃ devānāmānandaḥ | ye karmaṇā devānapiyanti | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ karmadevānāṃ devānāmānandāḥ | sa eko devānāmānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ devānāmānandāḥ | sa eka indrasyā''nandaḥ  śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śatamindrasyā''nandāḥ | sa eko bṛhaspaterānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ bṛhaspaterānandāḥ | sa ekaḥ prajāpaterānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya, iti | te ye śataṃ prajāpaterānandāḥ | sa eko brahmaṇa ānandaḥ | śrotriyasya cākāmahatasya , iti | sa yaścāyaṃ puruṣe | yaścāsāvāditye | sa ekaḥ, iti | sa ya evaṃvit | asmāllokātpretya | etamannamayamātmānamupasaṃkrāmati | etaṃ prāṇamayamātmānamupasaṃkrāmati | etaṃ manomayamātmānamupasaṃkrāmati | etaṃ vijñānamayamātmānamupasaṃkrāmati | etamānandamayamātmānamupasaṃkrāmati, iti |
tadapyeṣa śloko bhavati ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake'ṣṭamo'nuvākaḥ || 8 ||
atha navamo'nuvākaḥ
yato vāco nivartante | aprāpya manasā saha | ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān | na bibheti kutaścaneti, iti | etaṃ ha vāva na tapati | kimahaṃ sādhu nākaravam | kimahaṃ pāpamakaravamiti, iti | sa ya evaṃ vidvānete ātmānaṃ spṛṇute, iti | ubhe hyevaiṣa ete ātmānaṃ spṛṇute | ya eva veda, iti |
ityupaniṣat ||
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamaprapāṭhake navamo'nuvākaḥ samāptaḥ || 9 ||
brahmavididamekaviṃśatirannādannarasamayātprāṇo vyāno'pāna ākāśaḥ pṛthivī pucchaṃ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ prāṇaṃ yajurṛksāmā''deśo'tharvāṅgirasaḥ pucchaṃ dvāviṃtiryataḥ śraddhartaṃ satyaṃ yogo maho'ṣṭādaśa vijñānaṃ priyaṃ modaḥ pramoda ānando brahma pucchaṃ dvāviṃśatirasannevāṣṭāviṃśatirasatṣoḍaśa bhīṣā'smādekapañcāśadyataḥ kutaścaikādaśa |
saha nāvavatu | saha nau bhunaktu | saha vīryaṃ karavāvahai |
tejasvi nāvadhītamastu mā vidviṣāvahai ||
oṃ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ śāntiḥ |
hariḥ oṃ |
iti kṛṣṇayajurvedīyataittirīyāraṇyake'ṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ || 8 ||
